      e.hands.forEach(function (x, i) { var h = view.hands[i]; if (h) { h.res = x.res; h.ret = x.ret; } });
      drawLabels();
      setBal(e.bal);
      if (e.natural) { setBanner({ t: 'Blackjack', a: signed(e.net), cls: 'bj', pop: true }); sfx.bj(); sparks(); }
      else if (e.net > 0) { setBanner({ t: 'You win', a: signed(e.net), cls: 'win', pop: true }); sfx.win(); }
      else if (e.net < 0) { setBanner({ t: e.hands.length === 1 && e.hands[0].res === 'surrender' ? 'Surrendered' : 'You lose', a: signed(e.net), cls: 'lose', pop: true }); sfx.lose(); }
      else { setBanner({ t: 'Push', a: 'Bet returned', pop: true }); sfx.push(); }
      drawStrip();
    }
  };
  async function play(events) {
    busy = true; sync();
    for (var i = 0; i < events.length; i++) {
      var h = handlers[events[i].t];
      if (h) await h(events[i]);
    }
    busy = false;
    setBal(S.profile.balance);
    sync();
    updateVip();
    if (pendingVip && !BJ.inRound(S)) { var tier = pendingVip; pendingVip = 0; showLevelUp(tier); }
  }

  /* ---------- Controls ---------- */
  function chipsVal(id) { var v = parseInt(String($(id).value).replace(/[^\d]/g, ''), 10); return isNaN(v) ? 0 : v; }
  function setBet(v) {
    v = Math.max(0, Math.floor(v));
    $('#bet').value = v ? String(v) : '';
    S.settings.bet = v;
    clearErr();
    betChanged();
  }
  function clearErr() { $('#err').hidden = true; $('#betRow').classList.remove('bad'); }
  function showErr(m) { $('#err').textContent = m; $('#err').hidden = false; $('#betRow').classList.add('bad'); }
  function errText(code) {
    var t = BJ.tierOf(S), i = BJ.tierIndex(S.profile.wagered), n = TIERS[i + 1];
    switch (code) {
      case 'min': return 'Enter a bet of at least 1 chip.';
      case 'max': return 'Your ' + t.name + ' limit is ' + int(t.maxBet) + '.' + (n ? ' Reach ' + n.name + ' to raise it.' : '');
      case 'side': return 'A side bet can’t be larger than your main bet.';
      case 'funds': return 'You don’t have enough chips for this bet. Lower it or open Rewards.';
      default: return 'Bets must be whole chips.';
    }
  }
  function onDeal() {
    if (busy || BJ.inRound(S)) return;
    ac();
    if (BJ.canRefill(S)) {
      var amt = BJ.refill(S);
      persist(); setBal(S.profile.balance); toast('Added ' + fmt(amt) + ' chips', true); sfx.claim();
      if (S.settings.bet * C > S.profile.balance) setBet(Math.floor(S.profile.balance / C));
      sync();
      return;
    }
    var bet = chipsVal('#bet'), pp = chipsVal('#pp'), t3 = chipsVal('#t3');
    S.settings.bet = bet; S.settings.pp = pp; S.settings.t3 = t3;
    var res = BJ.start(S, bet * C, pp * C, t3 * C);
    if (!res.ok) { showErr(errText(res.err)); return; }
    clearErr();
    persist();
    sfx.chip();
    var fromPreview = !view.hands.length;
    clearTable(true);
    view.hands = [mkHand(bet * C)];
    view.hands[0].noDrop = fromPreview;
    syncDOM(); drawLabels();
    setSide('pp', pp * C, 'live');
    setSide('t3', t3 * C, 'live');
    runAfterClear(res.events);
  }
  function runAfterClear(events) {
    busy = true; sync();
    wait(ms(60)).then(function () { return play(events); });
  }
  function onAct(a) {
    if (busy) return;
    var res = BJ.act(S, a);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    persist();
    if (a === 'hit' || a === 'stand') ac();
    play(res.events);
  }
  function onIns(take) {
    if (busy) return;
    var res = BJ.insurance(S, take);
    if (!res.ok) return;
    persist();
    play(res.events);
  }

  function sync() {
    var r = S.round, inR = BJ.inRound(S);
    var phase = r && r.phase === 'insurance' ? 'ins' : inR ? 'play' : 'idle';
    $('#app').dataset.phase = phase;
    var can = BJ.can(S), hint = S.settings.hints && !busy ? BJ.advise(S) : null;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-act]').forEach(function (b) {
      var a = b.dataset.act;
      b.disabled = busy || !can[a];
      b.classList.toggle('hint', hint === a);
    });
    var locked = busy || inR;
    ['#bet', '#pp', '#t3', '#half', '#dbl', '#ppMatch', '#t3Match', '#sideToggle', '#spotPP', '#spotT3'].forEach(function (s) { $(s).disabled = locked; });
    document.querySelectorAll('#chips .chip').forEach(function (c) { c.disabled = locked; });
    var refill = BJ.canRefill(S);
    var d = $('#deal');
    d.disabled = locked;
    d.textContent = refill ? 'Refill ' + int(BJ.tierOf(S).refill) + ' free chips' : 'Deal';
    $('#insbox').hidden = phase !== 'ins';
    var cost = r ? Math.floor(r.bet / 2) : 0;
    $('#insYes').disabled = busy || S.profile.balance < cost;
    $('#insNo').disabled = busy;
    $('#insNo').classList.toggle('hint', hint === 'decline');
    var sh = S.shoe, left = Math.max(0, 1 - sh.pos / sh.cut);
    $('#shoeBar').style.width = left * 100 + '%';
    sideSummary();
    if (sheet === 'vip') renderRewards();
  }
  function sideSummary() {
    var total = chipsVal('#pp') + chipsVal('#t3');
    $('#sideSum').textContent = total ? int(total) : 'Off';
  }
  function drawChips() {
    var max = BJ.tierOf(S).maxBet;
    var list = DENOMS.filter(function (d) { return d[0] <= max; }).slice(-6);
    $('#chips').innerHTML = list.map(function (d) { return '<button class="chip" style="--c:' + d[1] + '" data-v="' + d[0] + '" aria-label="Add ' + d[0] + '"><span>' + compact(d[0]) + '</span></button>'; }).join('');
    $('#limit').textContent = 'Limit ' + int(max);
  }
  function drawStrip() {
    $('#strip').innerHTML = S.profile.history.filter(function (h) { return !h.game || h.game === 'bj'; }).slice(0, 6).map(function (h) {
      var c = h.net > 0 ? 'w' : h.net < 0 ? 'l' : '';
      var a = Math.abs(h.net / C);
      return '<span class="pill ' + c + '">' + (h.net > 0 ? '+' : h.net < 0 ? '−' : '') + (a >= 1000 ? compact(Math.round(a / 100) * 100) : Math.round(a * 10) / 10) + '</span>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function claimable(now) {
    return BJ.levelBonus(S) > 0 || BJ.rakeAvailable(S) >= C || BJ.dailyInfo(S, now).ready || BJ.weeklyInfo(S, now).ready;
  }
  function updateVip() {
    var p = BJ.vipProgress(S);
    $('#vipGem').innerHTML = gem(p.index, 30);
    $('#vipName').textContent = p.tier.name;
    var b = $('#vipBar');
    b.style.width = p.pct + '%';
    b.parentNode.style.setProperty('--tier', p.tier.color);
    $('#rewDot').hidden = !claimable(Date.now());
    drawChips();
    sync2();
    eachMod('refresh');
    drawLobbyVip();
  }
  function sync2() { var locked = busy || BJ.inRound(S); document.querySelectorAll('#chips .chip').forEach(function (c) { c.disabled = locked; }); }
  function applyCosmetics() {
    var root = document.documentElement;
    root.dataset.felt = S.settings.felt;
    root.dataset.back = S.settings.back;
  }

  /* ---------- Sheets ---------- */
  var ICON_X = '<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="m3 3 10 10M13 3 3 13"/></svg>';
  var TITLES = { vip: 'VIP', stats: 'Stats', rules: 'Rules', settings: 'Settings' };
  function nav() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.rail [data-nav]').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', sheet ? b.dataset.nav === sheet : b.dataset.nav === 'lobby' && currentView === 'lobby'); });
    document.querySelectorAll('.rail [data-game]').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', !sheet && b.dataset.game === currentView); });
  }
  var VIEW_IDS = { lobby: '#viewLobby', bj: '#viewBj', bac: '#viewBac', rou: '#viewRou', trade: '#viewTrade' };
  function show(name) {
    if (!VIEW_IDS[name]) name = 'lobby';
    Object.keys(VIEW_IDS).forEach(function (k) { $(VIEW_IDS[k]).hidden = k !== name; });
    currentView = name;
    $('#app').dataset.view = name;
    S.settings.view = name;
    if (KEY) { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(S)); } catch (e) {} }
    if (sheet) closeSheet(); else nav();
    if (name === 'lobby') renderLobby();
    modules.forEach(function (m) { if (m.id === name && m.enter) m.enter(); });
    if (name === 'bj' && !busy) restoreView();
  }
  function closeSheet() {
    sheet = null; clearInterval(sheetTimer);
    $('#sheetRoot').innerHTML = '';
    nav();
  }
  function openSheet(name) {
    sheet = name;
    clearInterval(sheetTimer);
    $('#sheetRoot').innerHTML = '<div class="scrim" id="scrim"><div class="sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="sheetTitle"><header><h2 id="sheetTitle">' + TITLES[name] + '</h2><button class="x" id="sheetX" aria-label="Close">' + ICON_X + '</button></header><div id="sheetBody"></div></div></div>';
    $('#sheetX').onclick = closeSheet;
    $('#scrim').onclick = function (e) { if (e.target.id === 'scrim') closeSheet(); };
    ({ vip: renderVip, stats: renderStats, rules: renderRules, settings: renderSettings })[name]();
    nav();
    if (name === 'vip') sheetTimer = setInterval(renderRewards, 1000);
  }
  function countdown(msLeft) {
    var s = Math.ceil(msLeft / 1000), d = Math.floor(s / 86400), h = Math.floor(s / 3600) % 24, m = Math.floor(s / 60) % 60, x = s % 60;
    if (d) return d + 'd ' + h + 'h';
    return h + ':' + String(m).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(x).padStart(2, '0');
  }
  function rewardRow(id, title, sub, amt, enabled, label) {
    return '<div class="reward"><div class="i"><b>' + title + '</b><span class="sub">' + sub + '</span></div>' + (amt ? '<span class="amt">' + amt + '</span>' : '') + '<button class="claim" data-claim="' + id + '"' + (enabled ? '' : ' disabled') + '>' + label + '</button></div>';
  }
  function renderRewards() {
    var box = $('#rewards');
    if (!box) return;
    var now = Date.now(), tier = BJ.tierOf(S), i = BJ.tierIndex(S.profile.wagered);
    var lv = BJ.levelBonus(S), rk = BJ.rakeAvailable(S), dy = BJ.dailyInfo(S, now), wk = BJ.weeklyInfo(S, now);
    var silver = TIERS[2];
    var dSub = dy.streak > 1 ? 'Day ' + dy.streak + ' streak, +' + (dy.streak - 1) * 10 + '%' : 'Claim on back-to-back days for up to +60%';
    var html =
      rewardRow('level', 'Level-up bonus', lv ? 'Paid once for each new tier' : 'Paid when you reach ' + (TIERS[i + 1] ? TIERS[i + 1].name : 'a new tier'), lv ? fmt(lv) : '', lv > 0, lv ? 'Claim' : 'None yet') +
      rewardRow('rake', 'Rakeback', tier.rake ? pctStr(tier.rake) + ' of every bet you place' : 'Starts at Bronze', tier.rake ? fmt(rk) : '', rk >= 1, tier.rake ? 'Claim' : 'Locked') +
      rewardRow('daily', 'Daily bonus', dSub, fmt(dy.amount), dy.ready, dy.ready ? 'Claim' : countdown(dy.left)) +
      rewardRow('weekly', 'Weekly bonus', wk.unlocked ? 'Grows with what you wager this week' : 'Starts at ' + silver.name, wk.unlocked ? fmt(wk.amount) : '', wk.ready, !wk.unlocked ? 'Locked' : wk.ready ? 'Claim' : countdown(wk.left)) +
      (BJ.canRefill(S) ? rewardRow('refill', 'Out of chips', 'Free refill while your balance is empty', fmt(tier.refill * C), true, 'Refill') : '');
    if (box.innerHTML !== html) box.innerHTML = html;
  }
  function renderVip() {
    var p = BJ.vipProgress(S), w = S.profile.wagered, ladder = '', felts = '', backs = '';
    TIERS.forEach(function (t, i) {
      var cur = i === p.index, lock = i > p.index;
      ladder += '<div class="tier' + (cur ? ' cur' : '') + (lock ? ' lock' : '') + '" style="--tier:' + t.color + '">' + gem(i, 34) +
        '<div class="nm">' + t.name + (cur ? '<span class="you">You</span>' : '') + '<span class="sub" style="margin-left:auto;font-weight:500">' + (t.wager ? 'Wager ' + compact(t.wager) : 'Start') + '</span></div>' +
        '<div class="perks"><div>' + (t.rake ? pctStr(t.rake) : 'None') + '<span>Rakeback</span></div><div>' + compact(t.maxBet) + '<span>Bet limit</span></div><div>' + compact(t.daily) + '<span>Daily</span></div><div>' + (t.bonus ? compact(t.bonus) : 'None') + '<span>Level bonus</span></div></div></div>';
      var fon = S.settings.felt === t.felt, bon = S.settings.back === t.back, b = BACKS[t.back];
      felts += '<button class="sw' + (fon ? ' on' : '') + '" data-felt="' + t.felt + '"' + (lock ? ' disabled' : '') + '><span class="prev" style="background:' + FELT_PREV[t.felt] + '"></span>' + (lock ? t.name : FELTS[t.felt]) + '</button>';
      backs += '<button class="sw' + (bon ? ' on' : '') + '" data-back="' + t.back + '"' + (lock ? ' disabled' : '') + '><span class="prev bk" style="--e:' + b[3] + ';background:repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,' + b[1] + ' 0 3px,' + b[2] + ' 3px 6px)"></span>' + (lock ? t.name : b[0]) + '</button>';
    });
    var toGo = p.next ? int(Math.ceil(p.toGo / C)) + ' more to ' + p.next.name : 'You have reached the top tier';
    $('#sheetBody').innerHTML =
      '<div class="vhero" style="--tier:' + p.tier.color + '">' + gem(p.index, 68) + '<div><h3>' + p.tier.name + '</h3><span class="bar"><i style="width:' + p.pct + '%"></i></span><span class="sub">Wagered ' + int(Math.floor(w / C)) + '. ' + toGo + '.</span></div></div>' +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>Rewards</h3><div id="rewards" style="display:grid;gap:6px"></div></div>' +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>Tiers</h3><div class="ladder">' + ladder + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>Tables</h3><p class="sub">Each tier unlocks a new table and card back.</p><div class="swatches">' + felts + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>Card backs</h3><div class="swatches">' + backs + '</div></div>';
    renderRewards();
  }
  function renderStats() {
    var st = S.profile.stats, h = S.profile.history;
    var rate = st.rounds ? Math.round((st.wins / st.rounds) * 100) : 0;
    var rows = h.map(function (x) {
      var cls = x.natural ? 'gold' : x.net > 0 ? 'pos' : '';
      var title = x.hands ? 'You ' + x.hands.map(function (y) { return y.v; }).join(', ') + ', dealer ' + x.dealer : x.label;
      var game = { bac: 'Baccarat', rou: 'Roulette' }[x.game] || 'Blackjack';
      return '<div class="hrow"><div>' + title + '<small>' + game + ', staked ' + fmt(x.staked) + '</small></div><span class="n ' + cls + '">' + signed(x.net) + '</span></div>';
    }).join('');
    var tile = function (l, v, c) { return '<div class="tile"><span>' + l + '</span><b class="' + (c || '') + '">' + v + '</b></div>'; };
    $('#sheetBody').innerHTML =
      '<div class="tiles">' + tile('Net profit', signed(st.net) || '0.00', st.net > 0 ? 'pos' : st.net < 0 ? 'neg' : '') + tile('Rounds', int(st.rounds)) + tile('Win rate', rate + '%') + tile('Blackjack hands', int((st.byGame || {}).bj || 0)) + tile('Baccarat hands', int((st.byGame || {}).bac || 0)) + tile('Roulette spins', int((st.byGame || {}).rou || 0)) + tile('Blackjacks', int(st.blackjacks)) + tile('Biggest win', fmt(st.biggestWin)) + tile('Best streak', int(st.bestStreak)) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>Profit over your last ' + Math.max(0, S.profile.curve.length - 1) + ' rounds</h3><div class="chart" id="chart"></div></div>' +
      rouTrends() + tradeTrends() +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>Recent rounds</h3><div class="hist">' + (rows || '<p class="sub">Your rounds will appear here once you play.</p>') + '</div></div>';
    drawChart($('#chart'), S.profile.curve);
  }
  function rouPill(n) { return '<span class="rn ' + BJ.rouColor(n) + '">' + n + '</span>'; }
  function rouTrends() {
    var h = BJ.rouHotCold(S, 100);
    if (!h.spins) return '';
    return '<div class="sec"><h3>Roulette numbers</h3><p class="sub">From your last ' + h.spins + ' spins. Every spin is independent.</p>' +
      '<div class="set"><div><b>Most frequent</b></div><div class="rnrow">' + h.hot.map(rouPill).join('') + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="set"><div><b>Least frequent</b></div><div class="rnrow">' + h.cold.map(rouPill).join('') + '</div></div></div>';
  }
  function tradeTrends() {
    var T = S.trade;
    if (!T || !T.count) return '';
    var tile = function (l, v, c) { return '<div class="tile"><span>' + l + '</span><b class="' + (c || '') + '">' + v + '</b></div>'; };
    return '<div class="sec"><h3>Trading</h3><div class="tiles">' + tile('Realized profit', signed(T.realized), T.realized > 0 ? 'pos' : T.realized < 0 ? 'neg' : '') + tile('Trades', int(T.count)) + tile('Liquidations', int(T.liqs || 0)) + '</div></div>';
  }
  function drawChart(el, data) {
    if (data.length < 2) { el.innerHTML = '<p class="sub" style="padding:40px 0;text-align:center">Play a round to start your profit chart.</p>'; return; }
    var W = 600, H = 170, L = 2, R = 2, T = 16, B = 16;
    var lo = Math.min(0, Math.min.apply(null, data)), hi = Math.max(0, Math.max.apply(null, data));
    if (lo === hi) hi = lo + C;
    var x = function (i) { return L + (i / (data.length - 1)) * (W - L - R); };
    var y = function (v) { return T + (1 - (v - lo) / (hi - lo)) * (H - T - B); };
    var d = data.map(function (v, i) { return (i ? 'L' : 'M') + x(i).toFixed(1) + ' ' + y(v).toFixed(1); }).join('');
    var col = data[data.length - 1] >= 0 ? '#27e07a' : '#ff5a6e', zero = y(0);
    el.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 ' + W + ' ' + H + '" preserveAspectRatio="none" role="img" aria-label="Line chart of net profit by round">' +
      '<defs><linearGradient id="fillg" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="' + col + '" stop-opacity=".22"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="' + col + '" stop-opacity="0"/></linearGradient></defs>' +
      '<path d="' + d + 'L' + x(data.length - 1).toFixed(1) + ' ' + zero + 'L' + x(0) + ' ' + zero + 'Z" fill="url(#fillg)"/>' +
      '<line x1="0" x2="' + W + '" y1="' + zero + '" y2="' + zero + '" stroke="#94aaba" stroke-opacity=".4" stroke-dasharray="4 4" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>' +
      '<path d="' + d + '" fill="none" stroke="' + col + '" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>' +
      '<line id="cross" x1="0" x2="0" y1="0" y2="' + H + '" stroke="#edf3f7" stroke-opacity=".5" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke" visibility="hidden"/>' +
      '</svg><div class="tip" id="tip" hidden></div>' +
      '<div class="sub" style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:2px"><span>Low ' + signed(lo) + '</span><span>High ' + signed(hi) + '</span></div>';
    var svg = el.querySelector('svg'), cross = el.querySelector('#cross'), tip = el.querySelector('#tip');
    function move(e) {
      var rect = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
      var f = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width));
      var i = Math.round(f * (data.length - 1));
      var px = x(i);
      cross.setAttribute('x1', px); cross.setAttribute('x2', px); cross.setAttribute('visibility', 'visible');
      tip.hidden = false;
      tip.textContent = signed(data[i]) || '0.00';
      var l = (px / W) * rect.width + 10;
      tip.style.left = Math.max(44, Math.min(rect.width - 24, l)) + 'px';
    }
    svg.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
    svg.addEventListener('pointerdown', move);
    svg.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () { cross.setAttribute('visibility', 'hidden'); tip.hidden = true; });
  }
  function payTable(rows) { return '<table class="pay">' + rows.map(function (r) { return '<tr><td>' + r[0] + '</td><td>' + r[1] + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>'; }
  function rulesBJ() {
    return '<div class="sec"><h3>Table rules</h3><ul class="rules">' +
      '<li>Six decks, reshuffled when the shoe reaches 75%.</li>' +
      '<li>Blackjack pays 3 to 2. Insurance pays 2 to 1.</li>' +
      '<li>The dealer stands on all 17s and peeks on aces and tens.</li>' +
      '<li>Double on any first two cards, including after a split.</li>' +
      '<li>Split up to four hands. Split aces get one card each and can’t be split again.</li>' +
      '<li>21 after a split pays even money.</li>' +
      '<li>Surrender gives back half your bet on your first decision.</li></ul></div>' +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>Perfect Pairs</h3><p class="sub">Your first two cards.</p>' + payTable([['Perfect pair (same suit)', '25 to 1'], ['Colored pair (same color)', '12 to 1'], ['Mixed pair', '6 to 1']]) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>21+3</h3><p class="sub">Your first two cards plus the dealer’s up card.</p>' + payTable([['Suited trips', '100 to 1'], ['Straight flush', '40 to 1'], ['Three of a kind', '30 to 1'], ['Straight', '10 to 1'], ['Flush', '5 to 1']]) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="sec"><h3>Keyboard</h3><p class="sub"><kbd>Space</kbd> deal, <kbd>H</kbd> hit, <kbd>S</kbd> stand, <kbd>D</kbd> double, <kbd>P</kbd> split, <kbd>R</kbd> surrender</p></div>';
  }
  function renderRules(tab) {
    tab = tab || (currentView === 'lobby' ? 'bj' : currentView);
    var names = { bj: 'Blackjack', bac: 'Baccarat', rou: 'Roulette', trade: 'Trading' };
    var mod = modules.filter(function (m) { return m.id === tab; })[0];
    var bar = '<div class="seg">' + Object.keys(names).map(function (k) { return '<button data-rules="' + k + '" class="' + (k === tab ? 'on' : '') + '">' + names[k] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
    $('#sheetBody').innerHTML = bar + (tab === 'bj' ? rulesBJ() : mod.rules()) + '<p class="sub">Empire Mobile Casino uses play chips only. They can’t be bought, sold or cashed out.</p>';
    $('#sheetBody').scrollTop = 0;
  }
  function renderSettings() {
    var tog = function (k, t, sub) { return '<div class="set"><div><b>' + t + '</b><span class="sub">' + sub + '</span></div><button class="tog" role="switch" aria-label="' + t + '" aria-checked="' + !!S.settings[k] + '" data-tog="' + k + '"></button></div>'; };
    var acct = Casino.accountSettings ? Casino.accountSettings() : '<div class="set"><div><b>Your progress</b><span class="sub">' + (remote ? 'Saves automatically to your Claude account' : 'Saves on this device only') + '</span></div></div>';
    $('#sheetBody').innerHTML = '<div class="sec">' + acct +
      tog('sound', 'Sound', 'Card, chip and win sounds') +
      tog('fast', 'Fast deal', 'Halves animation time') +
      tog('hints', 'Strategy hints', 'Outlines the basic strategy move in gold') +
      (Casino.embed ? '' : '<div class="set"><div><b>Reset progress</b><span class="sub">Clears chips, VIP tier and stats for good</span></div><button class="danger" id="resetBtn">Reset</button></div>') + '</div>';
  }

  function claim(id) {
    var now = Date.now(), amt = 0;
    if (id === 'level') { amt = BJ.claimLevel(S); }
    else if (id === 'rake') amt = BJ.claimRake(S);
    else if (id === 'daily') amt = BJ.claimDaily(S, now);
    else if (id === 'weekly') amt = BJ.claimWeekly(S, now);
    else if (id === 'refill') amt = BJ.refill(S);
    if (!amt) return;
    ac();
    persist();
    if (!busy) setBal(S.profile.balance);
    sfx.claim();
    toast('Added ' + fmt(amt) + ' chips', true);
    renderRewards();
    updateVip();
    sync();
  }

  function showLevelUp(i) {
    var t = TIERS[i], amt = BJ.levelBonus(S);
    sfx.level();
    drawChips();
    $('#lvlRoot').innerHTML = '<div class="lvl" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="lvlT"><div class="lvl-card" style="--tier:' + t.color + '"><div class="rays"></div>' + gem(i, 104) +
      '<div><p class="sub">VIP tier reached</p><h2 id="lvlT">' + t.name + '</h2></div>' +
      '<div class="lvl-perks"><div><span>Rakeback</span><b>' + pctStr(t.rake) + '</b></div><div><span>Bet limit</span><b>' + int(t.maxBet) + '</b></div><div><span>Daily bonus</span><b>' + int(t.daily) + '</b></div><div><span>New table and card back</span><b>' + FELTS[t.felt] + '</b></div></div>' +
      '<button class="go" id="lvlClaim">Claim ' + fmt(amt) + '</button><button class="later" id="lvlLater">Later</button></div></div>';
    $('#lvlClaim').onclick = function () { claim('level'); $('#lvlRoot').innerHTML = ''; };
    $('#lvlLater').onclick = function () { $('#lvlRoot').innerHTML = ''; };
    $('#lvlClaim').focus();
  }

  /* ---------- Restore ---------- */
  function applyAll() {
    applyCosmetics();
    setBal(S.profile.balance);
    $('#bet').value = S.settings.bet ? String(S.settings.bet) : '';
    $('#pp').value = S.settings.pp ? String(S.settings.pp) : '';
    $('#t3').value = S.settings.t3 ? String(S.settings.t3) : '';
    updateVip(); drawStrip(); sync();
  }
  function restoreView() {
    allCards().forEach(function (c) { c.el.remove(); });
    Array.prototype.slice.call($('#hands').children).forEach(function (n) { n.remove(); });
    view = { dealer: [], hands: [], active: -1 };
    setBanner(null);
    var r = S.round;
    if (r) {
      var settled = r.phase === 'settled';
      r.dealer.forEach(function (code, i) {
        var c = mkCard(code, i === 0 || r.holeUp);
        if (c.up) c.el.classList.add('up');
        view.dealer.push(c);
      });
      r.hands.forEach(function (h) {
        var vh = mkHand(h.bet);
        h.cards.forEach(function (code) { var c = mkCard(code, true); c.el.classList.add('up'); vh.cards.push(c); });
        vh.res = settled ? h.res : (h.bust ? 'lose' : h.surr ? 'surrender' : null);
        vh.ret = h.ret || 0;
        view.hands.push(vh);
      });
      view.active = r.phase === 'player' ? r.active : -1;
      if (settled) {
        if (r.hands.some(function (h) { return h.res === 'blackjack'; })) setBanner({ t: 'Blackjack', a: signed(r.net), cls: 'bj' });
        else if (r.net > 0) setBanner({ t: 'You win', a: signed(r.net), cls: 'win' });
        else if (r.net < 0) setBanner({ t: 'You lose', a: signed(r.net), cls: 'lose' });
        else setBanner({ t: 'Push', a: 'Bet returned' });
      } else if (r.phase === 'insurance') {
        setBanner({ t: 'Dealer shows an ace', a: 'Insurance?' });
        $('#insText').textContent = 'Insurance costs ' + fmt(Math.floor(r.bet / 2)) + ' and pays 2 to 1 if the dealer has blackjack.';
      }
    }
    instant = true;
    layout(0); drawLabels();
    ['pp', 't3'].forEach(function (k) {
      if (!r) return;
      var amt = r[k], res = r.side[k];
      if (!res) setSide(k, amt, 'live');
      else setSide(k, amt, res.win ? 'win' : 'lose', res.win ? res.win - amt : 0, res.kind ? BJ.SIDE_NAMES[res.kind] : null);
    });
    drawPreview();
    instant = false;
    sync();
  }

  /* ---------- Events ---------- */
  function bind() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.coinslot').forEach(function (n) { n.innerHTML = COIN; n.style.display = 'flex'; });
    $('#deal').onclick = onDeal;
    $('#half').onclick = function () { setBet(Math.max(1, Math.floor(chipsVal('#bet') / 2))); sfx.chip(); };
    $('#dbl').onclick = function () {
      var cap = Math.min(BJ.tierOf(S).maxBet, Math.floor(S.profile.balance / C));
      setBet(Math.max(1, Math.min(cap, chipsVal('#bet') * 2))); sfx.chip();
    };
    $('#chips').onclick = function (e) {
      var b = e.target.closest('.chip'); if (!b || b.disabled) return;
      ac();
      var cap = Math.min(BJ.tierOf(S).maxBet, Math.floor(S.profile.balance / C));
      var before = chipsVal('#bet');
      setBet(Math.min(cap, before + (+b.dataset.v)));
      if (chipsVal('#bet') > before) flyChip(b.getBoundingClientRect(), b, $('#hands .slot .stack'));
      sfx.chip();
    };
    ['#bet', '#pp', '#t3'].forEach(function (id) {
      $(id).addEventListener('input', function (e) {
        e.target.value = e.target.value.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
        clearErr(); sideSummary(); betChanged();
      });
    });
    $('#sideToggle').onclick = function () {
      var box = $('#sideBox'), open = box.hidden;
      box.hidden = !open; this.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open);
    };
    $('#ppMatch').onclick = function () { $('#pp').value = chipsVal('#pp') ? '' : String(chipsVal('#bet') || ''); sideSummary(); clearErr(); betChanged(); };
    $('#t3Match').onclick = function () { $('#t3').value = chipsVal('#t3') ? '' : String(chipsVal('#bet') || ''); sideSummary(); clearErr(); betChanged(); };
    $('#spotPP').onclick = $('#spotT3').onclick = function () { var b = $('#sideBox'); if (b.hidden) $('#sideToggle').click(); $(this.id === 'spotPP' ? '#pp' : '#t3').focus(); };
    $('#actions').onclick = function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('[data-act]'); if (b && !b.disabled) { ac(); onAct(b.dataset.act); } };
    $('#insYes').onclick = function () { ac(); onIns(true); };
    $('#insNo').onclick = function () { ac(); onIns(false); };
    document.querySelector('.rail').onclick = function (e) {
      var g = e.target.closest('[data-game]');
      if (g) { show(g.dataset.game); return; }
      var b = e.target.closest('[data-nav]');
      if (!b) return;
      if (b.dataset.nav === 'lobby') show('lobby'); else openSheet(b.dataset.nav);
    };
    $('#brandBtn').onclick = function () { show('lobby'); };
    $('#viewLobby').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var g = e.target.closest('[data-game]');
      if (g) { ac(); show(g.dataset.game); return; }
      if (e.target.closest('#lobVip')) openSheet('vip');
    });
    $('#vipChip').onclick = function () { openSheet('vip'); };
    $('#rewardsBtn').onclick = function () { openSheet('vip'); };

    $('#sheetRoot').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      var c = e.target.closest('[data-claim]');
      if (c && !c.disabled) { claim(c.dataset.claim); return; }
      var rt = e.target.closest('[data-rules]');
      if (rt) { renderRules(rt.dataset.rules); return; }
      var f = e.target.closest('button[data-felt]');
      if (f && !f.disabled) { S.settings.felt = f.dataset.felt; applyCosmetics(); persist(); keepScroll(renderVip); return; }
      var b = e.target.closest('button[data-back]');
      if (b && !b.disabled) { S.settings.back = b.dataset.back; applyCosmetics(); persist(); keepScroll(renderVip); return; }
      var t = e.target.closest('[data-tog]');
      if (t) { var k = t.dataset.tog; S.settings[k] = !S.settings[k]; t.setAttribute('aria-checked', S.settings[k]); persist(); sync(); return; }
      var r = e.target.closest('#resetBtn');
      if (r) {
        if (!r.classList.contains('arm')) { r.classList.add('arm'); r.textContent = 'Confirm reset'; return; }
        S = BJ.createState(); persist(); closeSheet(); applyAll(); restoreView(); eachMod('restore'); show('lobby'); toast('Progress reset');
      }
    });
    function keepScroll(fn) { var b = $('#sheetBody'), y = b.scrollTop; fn(); b.scrollTop = y; }

    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { if ($('#lvlRoot').innerHTML) $('#lvlRoot').innerHTML = ''; else if (sheet) closeSheet(); return; }
      if (sheet || $('#lvlRoot').innerHTML || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return;
      var tag = e.target.tagName;
      if (tag === 'INPUT') return;
      if (currentView !== 'bj') {
        modules.forEach(function (m) { if (m.id === currentView && m.onKey) m.onKey(e); });
        return;
      }
      var k = e.key.toLowerCase(), map = { h: 'hit', s: 'stand', d: 'double', p: 'split', r: 'surrender' };
      if (map[k]) { ac(); onAct(map[k]); }
      else if ((k === ' ' || k === 'enter') && tag !== 'BUTTON') { e.preventDefault(); onDeal(); }
    });
    window.addEventListener('pagehide', function () { flushRemote(); });
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { if (document.hidden) flushRemote(); });
    setInterval(function () { if (S) $('#rewDot').hidden = !claimable(Date.now()); }, 30000);
  }

  /* ---------- Lobby ---------- */
  function lobbyCard(code, style) {
    var c = mkCard(code, true);
    c.el.classList.add('up');
    c.el.setAttribute('style', style);
    return c.el.outerHTML;
  }
  function drawLobbyVip() {
    var box = $('#lobVip');
    if (!box) return;
    var p = BJ.vipProgress(S), ready = claimable(Date.now());
    box.style.setProperty('--tier', p.tier.color);
    box.innerHTML = gem(p.index, 40) + '<span class="lv-text"><b>' + p.tier.name + '</b><span class="bar"><i style="width:' + p.pct + '%"></i></span><span class="sub">' + (p.next ? int(Math.ceil(p.toGo / C)) + ' more to ' + p.next.name : 'Top tier reached') + '</span></span>' + (ready ? '<span class="lv-ready">Rewards ready</span>' : '<span class="lv-open">VIP</span>');
  }
  function renderLobby() {
    var inHand = BJ.inRound(S);
    var mkArt = {
      bj: lobbyCard('Ah', '--cw:78px;--i:0;--off:0px;left:calc(50% - 74px);top:22px;rotate:-10deg;z-index:1') + lobbyCard('Ks', '--cw:78px;--i:0;--off:0px;left:calc(50% - 16px);top:14px;rotate:8deg;z-index:2'),
      bac: lobbyCard('9d', '--cw:78px;--i:0;--off:0px;left:calc(50% - 74px);top:14px;rotate:-6deg;z-index:1') + lobbyCard('Kc', '--cw:78px;--i:0;--off:0px;left:calc(50% - 26px);top:22px;rotate:5deg;z-index:2'),
      rou: '<canvas class="gwheel" width="420" height="420" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>'
    };
    var tile = function (id, name, sub, status) {
      return '<button class="gtile ' + id + '" data-game="' + id + '"><span class="gart">' + mkArt[id] + '</span><span class="gtext"><b>' + name + '</b><span class="sub">' + sub + '</span>' + (status ? '<span class="gstat">' + status + '</span>' : '') + '</span></button>';
    };
    $('#viewLobby').innerHTML = '<div class="lob-in"><header class="lob-head"><h1>Empire Mobile Casino</h1><p class="sub">' + (accountName ? 'Signed in as ' + accountName + '. ' : '') + 'One balance and one VIP tier across every table.</p></header>' +
      '<button class="lob-vip" id="lobVip"></button><div class="gtiles">' +
      tile('bj', 'Blackjack', 'Beat the dealer to 21. Blackjack pays 3 to 2.', inHand ? 'Hand in progress' : '') +
      tile('bac', 'Baccarat', 'Bet Player, Banker or Tie, with full scoreboards.', '') +
      tile('rou', 'Roulette', 'European single-zero wheel with every bet on the board.', '') +
      '<button class="gtile tradeTile" data-game="trade"><span class="gart" id="lobTradeArt"></span><span class="gtext"><b>Crypto, stocks and meme coins</b><span class="sub">Go long or short with up to 40x on a simulated market with live charts.</span></span></button>' +
      '</div><p class="sub lob-foot">Play chips only. They can’t be bought or cashed out.</p></div>';
    drawLobbyVip();
    var w = $('#viewLobby .gwheel');
    if (w && Casino.drawWheel) Casino.drawWheel(w);
    if (Casino.drawTradeArt) Casino.drawTradeArt($('#lobTradeArt'));
  }

  /* ---------- Shared betting for table games ---------- */
  function Book() { this.bets = {}; this.log = []; }
  Book.prototype.total = function () { var t = 0, b = this.bets; Object.keys(b).forEach(function (k) { t += b[k]; }); return t; };
  Book.prototype.check = function (total) {
    var t = BJ.tierOf(S), i = BJ.tierIndex(S.profile.wagered), n = TIERS[i + 1];
    if (total > t.maxBet * C) return 'Your ' + t.name + ' limit is ' + int(t.maxBet) + ' per round.' + (n ? ' Reach ' + n.name + ' to raise it.' : '');
    if (total > S.profile.balance) return 'You don’t have enough chips for that bet.';
    return null;
  };
  Book.prototype.add = function (id, amt) {
    var e = this.check(this.total() + amt);
    if (e) return e;
    this.bets[id] = (this.bets[id] || 0) + amt;
    this.log.push({ id: id, amt: amt });
    return null;
  };
  Book.prototype.undo = function () {
    var l = this.log.pop();
    if (!l) return false;
    this.bets[l.id] -= l.amt;
    if (this.bets[l.id] <= 0) delete this.bets[l.id];
    return true;
  };
  Book.prototype.clear = function () { this.bets = {}; this.log = []; };
  Book.prototype.load = function (obj) {
    var t = 0;
    Object.keys(obj).forEach(function (k) { t += obj[k]; });
    var e = this.check(t);
    if (e) return e;
    this.bets = Object.assign({}, obj);
    var self = this;
    this.log = Object.keys(obj).map(function (k) { return { id: k, amt: obj[k] }; });
    void self;
    return null;
  };
  Book.prototype.double = function () {
    var self = this, e = this.check(this.total() * 2);
    if (e) return e;
    var entries = Object.keys(this.bets).map(function (k) { return { id: k, amt: self.bets[k] }; });
    entries.forEach(function (x) { self.bets[x.id] += x.amt; self.log.push(x); });
    return null;
  };

  function betPanel(root, o) {
    root.innerHTML = '<div><div class="lab"><span>Chip value</span><span>Total bet <b class="cp-total">0</b></span></div><div class="chips selc"></div></div>' +
      '<div class="cp-row"><button class="act sm" data-c="undo">Undo</button><button class="act sm" data-c="clear">Clear</button><button class="act sm" data-c="double">2×</button><button class="act sm" data-c="rebet">Rebet</button></div>' +
      '<button class="go cp-main"></button><p class="foot">Play chips only. They can’t be bought or cashed out.</p>';
    var bar = root.querySelector('.chips'), main = root.querySelector('.cp-main');
    function list() { var max = BJ.tierOf(S).maxBet; return DENOMS.filter(function (d) { return d[0] <= max; }).slice(-6); }
    function value() {
      var l = list(), v = S.settings.chip;
      if (!l.some(function (d) { return d[0] === v; })) { v = l[Math.min(2, l.length - 1)][0]; S.settings.chip = v; }
      return v;
    }
    function drawBar() {
      var v = value();
      bar.innerHTML = list().map(function (d) { return '<button class="chip' + (d[0] === v ? ' sel' : '') + '" style="--c:' + d[1] + '" data-v="' + d[0] + '" aria-label="Use ' + d[0] + ' chip" aria-pressed="' + (d[0] === v) + '"><span>' + compact(d[0]) + '</span></button>'; }).join('');
    }
    bar.onclick = function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('.chip'); if (!b || b.disabled) return; ac(); S.settings.chip = +b.dataset.v; drawBar(); sfx.chip(); };
    root.addEventListener('click', function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('[data-c]'); if (b && !b.disabled) { ac(); o[b.dataset.c](); } });
    main.onclick = function () { ac(); o.main(); };
    if (S) drawBar();
    return {
      value: function () { return value() * C; },
      selected: function () { return bar.querySelector('.chip.sel'); },
      redraw: drawBar,
      set: function (st) {
        root.querySelector('.cp-total').textContent = plain(st.total);
        main.textContent = st.label; main.disabled = !!st.mainOff;
        root.querySelectorAll('[data-c]').forEach(function (b) { b.disabled = !!st.locked || !!(st.off && st.off[b.dataset.c]); });
        bar.querySelectorAll('.chip').forEach(function (c) { c.disabled = !!st.locked; });
      }
    };
  }
  function doRefill() {
    var amt = BJ.refill(S);
    if (!amt) return;
    persist(); setBal(S.profile.balance); toast('Added ' + fmt(amt) + ' chips', true); sfx.claim(); updateVip(); sync();
  }
  function afterRound() {
    setBal(S.profile.balance);
    updateVip();
    if (pendingVip && !BJ.inRound(S)) { var t = pendingVip; pendingVip = 0; showLevelUp(t); }
  }

  function start(hot) {
    var saved = null;
    if (hot && hot.json) saved = parseSave(hot.json);
    if (!saved && KEY) { try { saved = parseSave(localStorage.getItem(KEY)); } catch (e) {} }
    S = saved || BJ.createState();
    bind();
    applyAll();
    restoreView();
    if (window.claude && window.claude.hot && window.claude.hot.snapshot) window.claude.hot.snapshot(function () { return { json: JSON.stringify(S) }; });
    connectRemote();
    if (!window.claude) setTimeout(connectRemote, 1500);
    eachMod('restore');
    var v = S.settings.view;
    if (BJ.inRound(S)) v = 'bj';
    show(VIEW_IDS[v] ? v : 'lobby');
    window.__casino = { get state() { return S; }, restoreView: function () { restoreView(); eachMod('restore'); }, applyAll: applyAll, persist: persist, show: show };
  }

  window.Casino = {
    get S() { return S; },
    show: show, refreshAll: function () { applyAll(); restoreView(); eachMod('restore'); renderLobby(); },
    register: function (m) { modules.push(m); if (m.init) m.init(); },
    setAccount: function (name, key, store) { accountName = name; KEY = key; remote = store; },
    isDirty: function () { return dirty || writing; },
    flushSoon: function () { dirty = true; clearTimeout(remoteTimer); remoteTimer = setTimeout(flushRemote, 300); },
    flushNow: async function () {
      clearTimeout(remoteTimer);
      for (var i = 0; i < 40 && writing; i++) await wait(50);
      if (dirty) await flushRemote();
    },
    adoptSave: async function (json) {
      var l = json ? parseSave(json) : BJ.createState();
      if (!l) return false;
      while (anyBusy()) await wait(120);
      S = l;
      if (KEY && json) { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, json); } catch (e) {} }
      applyAll(); restoreView(); eachMod('restore'); renderLobby();
      return true;
    },
    boot: function () {
      if (Casino.accountBoot) { Casino.accountBoot(start); return; }
      var hot = window.claude && window.claude.hot;
      if (hot && hot.ready) hot.ready(start); else start((hot && hot.data) || {});
    },
    persist: persist, setBal: setBal, toast: toast, sfx: sfx, tone: tone, snap: snap, fmt: fmt, int: int, compact: compact, signed: signed, plain: plain, signedPlain: signedPlain,
    ms: ms, wait: wait, reduced: reduced, mkCard: mkCard, showUp: showUp, stackHTML: stackHTML, drop: drop, flyAway: flyAway, flyChip: flyChip, DENOMS: DENOMS,
    Book: Book, betPanel: betPanel, doRefill: doRefill, afterRound: afterRound, noteVip: function (t) { pendingVip = Math.max(pendingVip, t); }, view: function () { return currentView; }, ac: ac, updateVip: updateVip
  };
})();

</script>
<script>
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var A = Casino, C = BJ.C;
  var root, book, panel, busy = false, phase = 'bet';
  var spots = {}, side = { p: null, b: null };
  var IDS = ['pp', 'tie', 'bp', 'player', 'banker'];
  var INFO = {
    pp: ['Player pair', '11 to 1'], tie: ['Tie', '8 to 1'], bp: ['Banker pair', '11 to 1'],
    player: ['Player', '1 to 1'], banker: ['Banker', '0.95 to 1']
  };
  function q(s) { return root.querySelector(s); }

  function build() {
    root = document.querySelector('#viewBac');
    root.innerHTML =
      '<section class="table bt" aria-label="Baccarat table">' +
        '<div class="broad"><div class="bcount"></div><div class="rd bead" aria-label="Bead plate"><div class="rdin"></div></div><div class="rd big" aria-label="Big road"><div class="rdin"></div></div></div>' +
        '<div class="bcards">' +
          '<div class="bhand player"><div class="blab"><span>Player</span><b class="btot"></b></div><div class="cards"></div></div>' +
          '<div class="bshoe" title="Cards left before the shuffle"><div class="shoe-cards" id="bacShoe"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div><span class="bar"><i id="bacShoeBar" style="width:100%"></i></span></div>' +
          '<div class="bhand banker"><div class="blab"><span>Banker</span><b class="btot"></b></div><div class="cards"></div></div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<div class="bspots">' + IDS.map(function (id) {
          return '<button class="bspot ' + id + '" data-b="' + id + '"><span class="bs-t"><b>' + INFO[id][0] + '</b><small>' + INFO[id][1] + '</small></span><span class="bs-c"><span class="stacks"></span><span class="amt"></span></span></button>';
        }).join('') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="banner" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>' +
      '</section><aside class="panel" aria-label="Baccarat controls"></aside>';
    IDS.forEach(function (id) {
      var el = q('.bspot.' + id);
      spots[id] = { el: el, stacks: el.querySelector('.stacks'), amt: el.querySelector('.amt'), sig: null };
    });
    side.p = { el: q('.bhand.player'), box: q('.bhand.player .cards'), tot: q('.bhand.player .btot'), cards: [] };
    side.b = { el: q('.bhand.banker'), box: q('.bhand.banker .cards'), tot: q('.bhand.banker .btot'), cards: [] };
    book = new A.Book();
    panel = A.betPanel(q('.panel'), { undo: onUndo, clear: onClear, double: onDouble, rebet: onRebet, main: onMain });
    q('.bspots').addEventListener('click', function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('.bspot'); if (b) onSpot(b.dataset.b, b); });
  }

  /* Spots */
  function setSpot(id, state, ret, animate) {
    var sp = spots[id], amt = state === 'live' ? (book.bets[id] || 0) : (lastBets()[id] || 0), net = (ret || 0) - amt;
    var sig = [amt, state, ret || 0].join('|');
    if (sp.sig === sig) return;
    var old = sp.stacks.querySelector('.stack:not(.pay)');
    if (state === 'lose' && animate) A.flyAway(old, q('.bshoe'));
    var main = state === 'lose' ? 0 : amt, pay = state === 'win' ? net : 0;
    sp.stacks.innerHTML = A.stackHTML(main) + (pay ? A.stackHTML(pay, 'pay') : '');
    sp.amt.textContent = !amt ? '' : state === 'win' ? A.signedPlain(net) : state === 'lose' ? A.signedPlain(-amt) : state === 'push' ? 'Push' : A.plain(amt);
    sp.amt.className = 'amt' + (state === 'win' ? ' won' : state === 'lose' ? ' lost' : '');
    sp.el.classList.toggle('win', state === 'win');
    if (animate) {
      if (state === 'live' && amt && !old) A.drop(sp.stacks.querySelector('.stack'));
      if (pay) A.drop(sp.stacks.querySelector('.pay'));
    }
    sp.sig = sig;
  }
  function lastBets() { var l = A.S.bacc.last; return l ? l.bets : {}; }
  function drawBets(animate) { IDS.forEach(function (id) { setSpot(id, 'live', 0, animate); }); }

  function startNew() {
    phase = 'bet';
    clearCards(true);
    setBanner(null);
    side.p.el.classList.remove('won'); side.b.el.classList.remove('won');
  }
  function onSpot(id, btn) {
    if (busy) return;
    A.ac();
    if (phase === 'result') { startNew(); book.clear(); }
    var err = book.add(id, panel.value());
    if (err) { A.toast(err); sync(); return; }
    drawBets(true);
    var target = spots[id].stacks.querySelector('.stack'), chip = panel.selected();
    if (target && chip) A.flyChip(chip.getBoundingClientRect(), chip, target);
    A.sfx.chip();
    sync();
    void btn;
  }
  function onUndo() { if (busy || phase === 'result') return; if (book.undo()) { drawBets(false); A.sfx.chip(); sync(); } }
  function onClear() { if (busy) return; if (phase === 'result') startNew(); book.clear(); drawBets(false); sync(); }
  function onDouble() {
    if (busy || phase === 'result') return;
    var err = book.double();
    if (err) { A.toast(err); return; }
    drawBets(true); A.sfx.chip(); sync();
  }
  function onRebet() {
    if (busy) return;
    var last = A.S.bacc.lastBets;
    if (!last) return;
    if (phase === 'result') startNew();
    var err = book.load(last);
    if (err) { A.toast(err); return; }
    drawBets(true); A.sfx.chip(); sync();
  }
  function onMain() {
    if (busy) return;
    var S = A.S;
    if (BJ.canRefill(S) && !book.total()) { A.doRefill(); sync(); return; }
    if (phase === 'result' && !book.total()) {
      var last = S.bacc.lastBets;
      if (!last) return;
      var err = book.load(last);
      if (err) { A.toast(err); return; }
    }
    if (!book.total()) return;
    var res = BJ.bacPlay(S, book.bets);
    if (!res.ok) { A.toast(res.err === 'funds' ? 'You don’t have enough chips for that bet.' : 'Check your bets and try again.'); return; }
    A.persist();
    A.sfx.chip();
    var wasResult = phase === 'result';
    phase = 'deal';
    if (wasResult) { clearCards(true); setBanner(null); side.p.el.classList.remove('won'); side.b.el.classList.remove('won'); }
    drawBetsFrom(res);
    run(res.events);
  }
  function drawBetsFrom() { IDS.forEach(function (id) { setSpot(id, 'live', 0, true); }); }

  /* Cards */
  function clearCards(animated) {
    ['p', 'b'].forEach(function (k) {
      var h = side[k];
      h.cards.forEach(function (c) {
        if (animated && !A.reduced) {
          var a = c.el.animate([{ opacity: 1, translate: '0 0' }, { opacity: 0, translate: '0 -30px' }], { duration: A.ms(200), fill: 'forwards' });
          a.onfinish = function () { c.el.remove(); };
        } else c.el.remove();
      });
      h.cards = [];
      h.tot.textContent = '';
    });
  }
  function addCard(k, code, idx, instant) {
    var h = side[k], c = A.mkCard(code, false);
    c.el.style.setProperty('--i', Math.min(idx, 1));
    c.el.style.zIndex = idx + 1;
    if (idx === 2) c.el.classList.add('third');
    h.box.appendChild(c.el);
    h.cards.push(c);
    if (instant) { c.up = true; c.el.classList.add('up'); return c; }
    var from = q('#bacShoe').getBoundingClientRect(), to = c.el.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (to.width && !A.reduced) {
      c.el.animate([{ translate: (from.left - to.left) + 'px ' + (from.top - to.top) + 'px', scale: String(from.width / to.width) }, { translate: '0 0', scale: '1' }], { duration: A.ms(340), easing: 'cubic-bezier(.18,.8,.25,1)' });
    }
    A.showUp(c);
    A.sfx.card();
    return c;
  }
  function setBanner(o) {
    var b = q('.banner');
    b.className = 'banner';
    if (!o) { b.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    b.innerHTML = '<div class="t">' + o.t + '</div>' + (o.a ? '<div class="a">' + o.a + '</div>' : '');
    if (o.cls) b.classList.add(o.cls);
    void b.offsetWidth;
    if (o.pop) b.classList.add('pop');
  }
  function drawShoe() {
    var sh = A.S.bacc.shoe;
    q('#bacShoeBar').style.width = Math.max(0, 1 - sh.pos / sh.cut) * 100 + '%';
  }

  /* Scoreboards */
  function bigRoad(road) {
    var cells = [], used = {}, col = -1, row = 0, start = -1, last = null, pending = 0;
    function key(c, r) { return c + ':' + r; }
    road.forEach(function (x) {
      if (x.w === 'T') {
        if (cells.length) cells[cells.length - 1].ties++; else pending++;
        return;
      }
      if (x.w !== last) {
        start++;
        while (used[key(start, 0)]) start++;
        col = start; row = 0;
      } else if (row < 5 && !used[key(col, row + 1)]) row++;
      else col++;
      used[key(col, row)] = 1;
      cells.push({ x: col, y: row, w: x.w, ties: pending, pp: x.pp, bp: x.bp });
      pending = 0;
      last = x.w;
    });
    return cells;
  }
  function drawRoads() {
    var road = A.S.bacc.road, n = { P: 0, B: 0, T: 0 };
    road.forEach(function (x) { n[x.w]++; });
    q('.bcount').innerHTML = '<span class="ct P">Player <b>' + n.P + '</b></span><span class="ct B">Banker <b>' + n.B + '</b></span><span class="ct T">Tie <b>' + n.T + '</b></span><span class="ct">Hands <b>' + road.length + '</b></span>';
    var dots = function (x) { return (x.bp ? '<i class="dt b"></i>' : '') + (x.pp ? '<i class="dt p"></i>' : ''); };
    var beadCols = Math.floor(Math.max(0, road.length - 1) / 6) + 1;
    var bead = q('.bead .rdin');
    bead.style.setProperty('--cols', Math.max(beadCols, 8));
    bead.innerHTML = road.map(function (x, i) {
      return '<span class="bd ' + x.w + '" style="--x:' + Math.floor(i / 6) + ';--y:' + (i % 6) + '">' + x.w + dots(x) + '</span>';
    }).join('');
    var cells = bigRoad(road), maxX = 0;
    cells.forEach(function (c) { if (c.x > maxX) maxX = c.x; });
    var big = q('.big .rdin');
    big.style.setProperty('--cols', Math.max(maxX + 2, 16));
    big.innerHTML = cells.map(function (c) {
      return '<span class="br ' + c.w + '" style="--x:' + c.x + ';--y:' + c.y + '">' + (c.ties ? '<em>' + (c.ties > 1 ? c.ties : '') + '</em>' : '') + dots(c) + '</span>';
    }).join('');
    [q('.bead'), q('.big')].forEach(function (s) { s.scrollLeft = s.scrollWidth; });
  }

  /* Round playback */
  async function run(events) {
    busy = true; sync();
    await A.wait(A.ms(160));
    for (var i = 0; i < events.length; i++) {
      var e = events[i];
      if (e.t === 'shuffle') { A.toast('New shoe shuffled'); drawRoads(); await A.wait(A.ms(300)); }
      else if (e.t === 'bal') A.setBal(e.v);
      else if (e.t === 'vipUp') A.noteVip(e.tier);
      else if (e.t === 'deal') {
        if (e.idx === 2) await A.wait(A.ms(380));
        addCard(e.to, e.card, e.idx);
        await A.wait(A.ms(170));
        side[e.to].tot.textContent = e.total;
        await A.wait(A.ms(170));
      } else if (e.t === 'settle') await settle(e.result);
    }
    busy = false; phase = 'result';
    book.clear();
    sync();
    A.afterRound();
  }
  function showResult(r, animate) {
    side.p.el.classList.toggle('won', r.winner === 'player');
    side.b.el.classList.toggle('won', r.winner === 'banker');
    IDS.forEach(function (id) {
      var amt = r.bets[id] || 0, ret = r.rets[id] || 0;
      if (!amt) { setSpot(id, 'live', 0, false); return; }
      setSpot(id, ret > amt ? 'win' : ret === amt ? 'push' : 'lose', ret, animate);
    });
    var title = r.winner === 'tie' ? 'Tie ' + r.pt + ' all' : (r.winner === 'player' ? 'Player wins ' + r.pt + ' to ' + r.bt : 'Banker wins ' + r.bt + ' to ' + r.pt);
    if (r.natural) title = 'Natural. ' + title;
    var cls = r.net > 0 ? 'win' : r.net < 0 ? 'lose' : '';
    setBanner({ t: title, a: r.net ? A.signed(r.net) : 'Bets returned', cls: cls, pop: animate });
  }
  async function settle(r) {
    await A.wait(A.ms(320));
    book.clear();
    showResult(r, true);
    A.setBal(A.S.profile.balance);
    if (r.net > 0) A.sfx.win(); else if (r.net < 0) A.sfx.lose(); else A.sfx.push();
    drawRoads(); drawShoe();
  }

  function sync() {
    var S = A.S, total = book.total(), locked = busy;
    var last = S.bacc.lastBets, refill = BJ.canRefill(S) && !total, label = 'Deal', off = false;
    if (refill) label = 'Refill ' + A.int(BJ.tierOf(S).refill) + ' free chips';
    else if (phase === 'result' && !total && last) label = 'Rebet and deal';
    else if (!total) { label = 'Place a bet'; off = true; }
    panel.set({
      total: phase === 'result' && !total ? 0 : total, label: label, mainOff: locked || off, locked: locked,
      off: { undo: phase === 'result' || !book.log.length, clear: !total && phase !== 'result', double: !total, rebet: !last }
    });
    drawShoe();
    root.querySelectorAll('.bspot').forEach(function (b) { b.disabled = locked; });
  }

  function restore() {
    if (busy) return;
    clearCards(false);
    book.clear();
    setBanner(null);
    side.p.el.classList.remove('won'); side.b.el.classList.remove('won');
    IDS.forEach(function (id) { spots[id].sig = null; });
    var l = A.S.bacc.last;
    if (l) {
      l.player.forEach(function (c, i) { addCard('p', c, i, true); });
      l.banker.forEach(function (c, i) { addCard('b', c, i, true); });
      side.p.tot.textContent = l.pt; side.b.tot.textContent = l.bt;
      phase = 'result';
      showResult(l, false);
    } else { phase = 'bet'; drawBets(false); }
    drawRoads();
    sync();
  }

  A.register({
    id: 'bac', init: build, restore: restore, isBusy: function () { return busy; },
    refresh: function () { panel.redraw(); if (A.S) sync(); },
    enter: function () { drawRoads(); sync(); },
    onKey: function (e) { if (e.key === ' ' && e.target.tagName !== 'BUTTON') { e.preventDefault(); onMain(); } },
    rules: function () {
      var row = function (a, b) { return '<tr><td>' + a + '</td><td>' + b + '</td></tr>'; };
      return '<div class="sec"><h3>How it works</h3><ul class="rules"><li>Bet on the Player or Banker hand finishing closest to 9, or on a tie.</li><li>Tens and face cards count 0. Aces count 1. Only the last digit of a total counts, so 7 + 8 = 5.</li><li>Eight decks, reshuffled near the end of the shoe. The scoreboards reset with each shoe.</li></ul></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>Payouts</h3><table class="pay">' + row('Player', '1 to 1') + row('Banker (5% commission)', '0.95 to 1') + row('Tie', '8 to 1') + row('Player pair or banker pair', '11 to 1') + '</table><p class="sub">On a tie, Player and Banker bets are returned.</p></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>Third card</h3><ul class="rules"><li>If either hand has 8 or 9 on two cards, both stand.</li><li>Player draws on 0 to 5 and stands on 6 or 7.</li><li>If Player stood, Banker draws on 0 to 5.</li></ul>' +
        '<table class="pay wrap">' + row('Banker 0 to 2', 'Always draws') + row('Banker 3', 'Draws unless Player’s third card is 8') + row('Banker 4', 'Draws on Player’s 2 to 7') + row('Banker 5', 'Draws on Player’s 4 to 7') + row('Banker 6', 'Draws on Player’s 6 or 7') + row('Banker 7', 'Stands') + '</table></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>Scoreboards</h3><ul class="rules"><li>The bead plate lists every hand in order, top to bottom.</li><li>The big road shows runs. A new column starts when the winner changes, and a green stroke marks a tie.</li><li>Past results don’t predict the next hand.</li></ul></div>';
    }
  });
})();

</script>
<script>
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var A = Casino, C = BJ.C, TAU = Math.PI * 2, WHEEL = BJ.WHEEL, STEP = TAU / 37;
  var root, book, panel, busy = false, phase = 'bet', skip = false, hideTimer = 0;
  var wheelEl, ballEl, numEl, wrap, board, nums, layer;
  var orient = 'v', mode = 'single', wheelAngle = 0, ballA = -Math.PI / 2, ballR = 0.93, resultBets = null;
  var verticalMq = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 860px) and (min-height: 521px)');
  var overlayMq = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 860px), (max-height: 520px)');
  var fineMq = window.matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)');
  var COLORS = { red: '#c9263d', black: '#16242e', green: '#0f9460' };
  var BALL_RATIO = 0.5 / 0.034;

  function q(s) { return root.querySelector(s); }

  /* ---------- Wheel ---------- */
  function drawWheel(cv) {
    var ctx = cv.getContext('2d'), W = cv.width, R = W / 2, i;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, W);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(R, R);
    function ring(r0, r1, a0, a1, fill) {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r1, a0, a1); ctx.arc(0, 0, r0, a1, a0, true); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.fill();
    }
    var rim = ctx.createRadialGradient(0, 0, R * 0.86, 0, 0, R);
    rim.addColorStop(0, '#0b1720'); rim.addColorStop(0.55, '#2b4556'); rim.addColorStop(0.8, '#1a2f3d'); rim.addColorStop(1, '#3a5a6e');
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * 0.995, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = rim; ctx.fill();
    ring(R * 0.885, R * 0.968, 0, TAU, '#102330');
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * 0.968, 0, TAU); ctx.lineWidth = R * 0.006; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.14)'; ctx.stroke();

    for (i = 0; i < 37; i++) {
      var a0 = -Math.PI / 2 + (i - 0.5) * STEP, a1 = a0 + STEP;
      ring(R * 0.6, R * 0.885, a0, a1, COLORS[BJ.rouColor(WHEEL[i])]);
    }
    ring(R * 0.6, R * 0.735, 0, TAU, 'rgba(0,0,0,.34)');
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#d9b45f';
    for (i = 0; i < 37; i++) {
      var a = -Math.PI / 2 + (i - 0.5) * STEP;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a) * R * 0.6, Math.sin(a) * R * 0.6); ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a) * R * 0.885, Math.sin(a) * R * 0.885);
      ctx.lineWidth = R * 0.005; ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.lineWidth = R * 0.008;
    [0.6, 0.735, 0.885].forEach(function (r) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); });

    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.font = '800 ' + Math.round(R * 0.078) + 'px Figtree, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (i = 0; i < 37; i++) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.rotate(i * STEP); ctx.fillText(String(WHEEL[i]), 0, -R * 0.81); ctx.restore();
    }

    var cone = ctx.createRadialGradient(-R * 0.14, -R * 0.18, R * 0.05, 0, 0, R * 0.6);
    cone.addColorStop(0, '#3f6276'); cone.addColorStop(0.6, '#20394a'); cone.addColorStop(1, '#10222e');
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * 0.6, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = cone; ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(217,180,95,.55)'; ctx.lineWidth = R * 0.006;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * 0.42, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
    for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.rotate(i * TAU / 8 + Math.PI / 8);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -R * 0.42); ctx.lineTo(R * 0.03, -R * 0.5); ctx.lineTo(0, -R * 0.58); ctx.lineTo(-R * 0.03, -R * 0.5); ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#d9b45f'; ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
    }
    var gold = ctx.createLinearGradient(-R * 0.2, -R * 0.2, R * 0.2, R * 0.2);
    gold.addColorStop(0, '#fbe3a0'); gold.addColorStop(0.5, '#d9a93f'); gold.addColorStop(1, '#8e6518');
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.rotate(i * Math.PI / 2);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(-R * 0.022, -R * 0.3, R * 0.044, R * 0.3, R * 0.02); ctx.fillStyle = gold; ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -R * 0.3, R * 0.04, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * 0.115, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = gold; ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, R * 0.06, 0, TAU); ctx.fillStyle = '#6f4d10'; ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  A.drawWheel = drawWheel;

  function placeWheel() {
    wheelEl.style.transform = 'rotate(' + wheelAngle + 'rad)';
    var x = Math.cos(ballA) * ballR * BALL_RATIO * 100, y = Math.sin(ballA) * ballR * BALL_RATIO * 100;
    ballEl.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + x + '%,' + y + '%,0)';
  }
  function ballAt(n) {
    ballA = wheelAngle - Math.PI / 2 + WHEEL.indexOf(n) * STEP;
    ballR = 0.665;
    placeWheel();
  }
  function smooth(x) { x = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x)); return x * x * (3 - 2 * x); }
  function animate(n, cb) {
    var dur = A.reduced ? 1 : A.ms(5600), i = WHEEL.indexOf(n), offset = -Math.PI / 2 + i * STEP;
    var w0 = wheelAngle % TAU, W = TAU * (1.5 + Math.random() * 0.6), wEnd = w0 + W, bEnd = wEnd + offset, turns = TAU * 8;
    var startR = ballR, t0 = performance.now(), nextTick = 0.6, lastBounce = 0;
    skip = false;
    function frame(now) {
      var t = skip ? 1 : Math.min(1, (now - t0) / dur);
      var e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3), wa = w0 + W * e;
      var be = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 2.6), b = bEnd + turns * (1 - be), lock = wa + offset;
      var a = b + (lock - b) * smooth((t - 0.78) / 0.14);
      var r;
      if (t < 0.06) r = startR + (0.93 - startR) * smooth(t / 0.06);
      else if (t < 0.58) r = 0.93;
      else if (t < 0.72) r = 0.93 - 0.18 * smooth((t - 0.58) / 0.14);
      else if (t < 0.92) {
        var tau = (t - 0.72) / 0.2, base = 0.75 - 0.085 * tau;
        var hop = Math.abs(Math.sin(Math.PI * 4 * tau)) * Math.pow(1 - tau, 2) * 0.075;
        r = base + hop;
        var bounce = Math.floor(tau * 4);
        if (bounce !== lastBounce) { lastBounce = bounce; A.tone(1500 - bounce * 150, 0, 0.05, 0.07, 'triangle'); }
      } else r = 0.665;
      if (t > nextTick && t < 0.72) { nextTick += 0.018; A.snap(0.08, 3200, 0.03); }
      wheelAngle = wa; ballA = a; ballR = r;
      placeWheel();
      if (t < 1) requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      else { ballAt(n); cb(); }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  }

  /* ---------- Board ---------- */
  function area(c0, r0, c1, r1) { return 'grid-area:' + r0 + '/' + c0 + '/' + (r1 + 1) + '/' + (c1 + 1); }
  function diamond(k) { return '<svg class="dm" viewBox="0 0 34 20" aria-label="' + k + '"><path d="M17 1 33 10 17 19 1 10z" fill="' + COLORS[k] + '" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1"/></svg>'; }
  var OUT_LABEL = { low: '1 to 18', even: 'Even', odd: 'Odd', high: '19 to 36', d1: '1st 12', d2: '2nd 12', d3: '3rd 12', c1: '2 to 1', c2: '2 to 1', c3: '2 to 1' };
  function outCell(id, st) {
    var inner = id === 'red' || id === 'black' ? diamond(id) : '<span class="otxt">' + OUT_LABEL[id] + '</span>';
    return '<div class="rcell out ' + id + '" data-id="' + id + '" style="' + st + '" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Bet ' + (OUT_LABEL[id] || id) + '">' + inner + '</div>';
  }
  function build() {
    orient = verticalMq.matches ? 'v' : 'h';
    board.className = 'rboard ' + orient;
    var h = '', n, i;
    var EVEN = ['low', 'even', 'red', 'black', 'odd', 'high'];
    var cells = '';
    for (n = 1; n <= 36; n++) {
      var col = (n - 1) % 3, row = Math.floor((n - 1) / 3);
      var st = orient === 'v' ? area(col + 1, row + 1, col + 1, row + 1) : area(row + 1, 3 - col, row + 1, 3 - col);
      cells += '<div class="rcell num ' + BJ.rouColor(n) + '" data-n="' + n + '" style="' + st + '"><span>' + n + '</span></div>';
    }
    var numsHtml = '<div class="rnums" id="rNums">' + cells + '<div class="rlayer"></div></div>';
    if (orient === 'v') {
      h += '<div class="rcell num green zero" data-id="i:0" data-n="0" style="' + area(3, 1, 5, 1) + '" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Bet 0"><span>0</span></div>';
      h += numsHtml.replace('class="rnums"', 'style="' + area(3, 2, 5, 13) + '" class="rnums"');
      for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) h += outCell('d' + (i + 1), area(1, 2 + i * 4, 1, 5 + i * 4));
      for (i = 0; i < 6; i++) h += outCell(EVEN[i], area(2, 2 + i * 2, 2, 3 + i * 2));
      for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) h += outCell('c' + (i + 1), area(3 + i, 14, 3 + i, 14));
    } else {
      h += '<div class="rcell num green zero" data-id="i:0" data-n="0" style="' + area(1, 1, 1, 3) + '" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-label="Bet 0"><span>0</span></div>';
      h += numsHtml.replace('class="rnums"', 'style="' + area(2, 1, 13, 3) + '" class="rnums"');
      for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) h += outCell('c' + (3 - i), area(14, 1 + i, 14, 1 + i));
      for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) h += outCell('d' + (i + 1), area(2 + i * 4, 4, 5 + i * 4, 4));
      for (i = 0; i < 6; i++) h += outCell(EVEN[i], area(2 + i * 2, 5, 3 + i * 2, 5));
    }
    board.innerHTML = h;
    nums = q('#rNums');
    layer = q('.rlayer');
    bindNums();
    drawChips();
  }

  /* Logical grid: a runs across a street (1 to 3), b runs along the table (1 to 34). */
  function logical(e) {
    var r = nums.getBoundingClientRect(), x = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width, y = (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height;
    if (x < 0 || x > 1 || y < 0 || y > 1) return null;
    var a = orient === 'v' ? x * 3 : (1 - y) * 3, b = orient === 'v' ? y * 12 : x * 12;
    var col = Math.min(2, Math.floor(a)), row = Math.min(11, Math.floor(b));
    return { col: col, row: row, fa: a - col, fb: b - row };
  }
  function numAt(col, row) { return row * 3 + col + 1; }
  function streetOf(row) { return [numAt(0, row), numAt(1, row), numAt(2, row)]; }
  function cornerNums(col, row, da, db) {
    if (db === -1 && row === 0) {
      if (col === 0 && da === -1) return [0, 1, 2, 3];
      if (col + da === -1 || col + da === 3) da = -da;
      var c0 = Math.min(col, col + da);
      return c0 === 0 ? [0, 1, 2] : [0, 2, 3];
    }
    var r0 = db === -1 ? row - 1 : row, c1 = da === -1 ? col - 1 : col;
    return [numAt(c1, r0), numAt(c1 + 1, r0), numAt(c1, r0 + 1), numAt(c1 + 1, r0 + 1)];
  }
  function pick(p) {
    var col = p.col, row = p.row, n = numAt(col, row), fa = p.fa, fb = p.fb, T = 0.24, da, db;
    if (mode === 'single') return [n];
    if (mode === 'street') return streetOf(row);
    if (mode === 'line') {
      var other = fb < 0.5 ? row - 1 : row + 1;
      if (other < 0) other = 1; if (other > 11) other = 10;
      return streetOf(Math.min(row, other)).concat(streetOf(Math.max(row, other)));
    }
    if (mode === 'corner') {
      da = fa < 0.5 ? -1 : 1; db = fb < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;
      if (col === 0 && da === -1 && !(row === 0 && db === -1)) da = 1;
      if (col === 2 && da === 1 && !(row === 0 && db === -1)) da = -1;
      if (row === 11 && db === 1) db = -1;
      return cornerNums(col, row, da, db);
    }
    if (mode === 'split') {
      var opts = [];
      if (col > 0) opts.push([fa, [n, n - 1]]);
      if (col < 2) opts.push([1 - fa, [n, n + 1]]);
      opts.push([fb, row > 0 ? [n, n - 3] : [0, n]]);
      if (row < 11) opts.push([1 - fb, [n, n + 3]]);
      opts.sort(function (x, y) { return x[0] - y[0]; });
      return opts[0][1];
    }
    /* Mouse: the exact spot under the cursor decides the bet. */
    da = fa < T ? -1 : fa > 1 - T ? 1 : 0;
    db = fb < T ? -1 : fb > 1 - T ? 1 : 0;
    if (row === 11 && db === 1) db = 0;
    if (col === 0 && da === -1) {
      if (row === 0 && db === -1) return [0, 1, 2, 3];
      if (db === 0) return streetOf(row);
      return streetOf(row + (db === -1 ? -1 : 0)).concat(streetOf(row + (db === -1 ? 0 : 1)));
    }
    if (col === 2 && da === 1) da = 0;
    if (da && db) return cornerNums(col, row, da, db);
    if (db === -1 && row === 0) return [0, n];
    if (db) return [n, n + db * 3];
    if (da) return [n, n + da];
    return [n];
  }
  function currentMode() { return fineMq.matches ? 'auto' : mode; }
  function pickFor(e) {
    var p = logical(e);
    if (!p) return null;
    var keep = mode;
    mode = currentMode();
    var r = pick(p);
    mode = keep;
    return r;
  }
  function highlight(list) {
    board.querySelectorAll('.hl').forEach(function (c) { c.classList.remove('hl'); });
    (list || []).forEach(function (n) { var c = board.querySelector('[data-n="' + n + '"]'); if (c) c.classList.add('hl'); });
  }
  function bindNums() {
    nums.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
      if (busy) return;
      if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' || e.buttons) highlight(pickFor(e));
    });
    nums.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () { highlight(null); });
    nums.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { if (!busy) { highlight(pickFor(e)); if (nums.releasePointerCapture) { try { nums.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (x) {} } } });
  }
  function bindBoard() {
    /* Pointer position, not click, so a phone tap near the zero can't be pulled onto the wrong bet. */
    board.addEventListener('pointerup', function (e) {
      if (busy) return;
      var el = document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX, e.clientY);
      if (!el) return;
      if (el.closest('#rNums')) {
        var list = pickFor(e);
        if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse') highlight(null);
        if (list) placeBet(BJ.insideId(list));
        return;
      }
      var c = el.closest('[data-id]');
      if (c) placeBet(c.dataset.id);
    });
    board.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
      if (e.key !== 'Enter' && e.key !== ' ') return;
      var c = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
      if (c && !busy) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); placeBet(c.dataset.id); }
    });
    board.addEventListener('mouseover', function (e) {
      var c = e.target.closest('[data-id]');
      if (busy) return;
      if (c) highlight(BJ.rouNums(c.dataset.id));
      else if (!e.target.closest('#rNums')) highlight(null);
    });
    board.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () { highlight(null); });
  }

  /* Where a chip sits, in grid units */
  function chipPos(id) {
    var list = BJ.rouNums(id), hasZero = list[0] === 0, nz = list.filter(Boolean);
    var cols = nz.map(function (n) { return (n - 1) % 3; }), rows = nz.map(function (n) { return Math.floor((n - 1) / 3); });
    var avg = function (a) { return a.reduce(function (x, y) { return x + y; }, 0) / a.length; };
    var a = avg(cols) + 0.5, b = avg(rows) + 0.5;
    if (hasZero) { b = 0; if (list.length === 4) a = 0; }
    else if (list.length === 3 || list.length === 6) a = 0;
    return orient === 'v' ? { x: a / 3, y: b / 12 } : { x: b / 12, y: 1 - a / 3 };
  }
  function chipHTML(amt, extra, style, id) {
    var d = A.DENOMS[0];
    A.DENOMS.forEach(function (x) { if (amt / C >= x[0]) d = x; });
    return '<span class="rchip' + (extra ? ' ' + extra : '') + '" data-bet="' + id + '" style="' + style + '"><span class="mini" style="--c:' + d[1] + ';--j:0"><b>' + A.compact(Math.floor(amt / C)) + '</b></span></span>';
  }
  function inside(id) { return id.indexOf('i:') === 0 && id !== 'i:0'; }
  function drawChips(wins) {
    if (!layer) return;
    board.querySelectorAll('.rchip').forEach(function (c) { c.remove(); });
    var src = wins || book.bets, html = '';
    Object.keys(src).forEach(function (id) {
      if (inside(id)) {
        var p = chipPos(id);
        html += chipHTML(src[id], wins ? 'won' : '', 'left:' + p.x * 100 + '%;top:' + p.y * 100 + '%', id);
      } else {
        var cell = board.querySelector('[data-id="' + id + '"]');
        if (cell) cell.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', chipHTML(src[id], wins ? 'won' : '', 'left:50%;top:50%', id));
      }
    });
    layer.innerHTML = html;
  }
  function chipEl(id) { return board.querySelector('.rchip[data-bet="' + id + '"]'); }

  /* ---------- Actions ---------- */
  function startNew() {
    phase = 'bet';
    resultBets = null;
    board.querySelectorAll('.hit').forEach(function (c) { c.classList.remove('hit'); });
    setBanner(null);
    numEl.hidden = true;
    book.clear();
    drawChips();
  }
  function placeBet(id) {
    A.ac();
    if (phase === 'result') startNew();
    var err = book.add(id, panel.value());
    if (err) { A.toast(err); sync(); return; }
    drawChips();
    var t = chipEl(id), chip = panel.selected();
    if (t && chip) A.flyChip(chip.getBoundingClientRect(), chip, t);
    A.sfx.chip();
    sync();
  }
  function onUndo() { if (busy || phase === 'result') return; if (book.undo()) { drawChips(); A.sfx.chip(); sync(); } }
  function onClear() { if (busy) return; startNew(); sync(); }
  function onDouble() {
    if (busy || phase === 'result') return;
    var err = book.double();
    if (err) { A.toast(err); return; }
    drawChips(); A.sfx.chip(); sync();
  }
  function onRebet() {
    if (busy) return;
    var last = A.S.roul.lastBets;
    if (!last) return;
    if (phase === 'result') startNew();
    var err = book.load(last);
    if (err) { A.toast(err); return; }
    drawChips(); A.sfx.chip(); sync();
  }
  function onMain() {
    if (busy) return;
    var S = A.S;
    if (BJ.canRefill(S) && !book.total()) { A.doRefill(); sync(); return; }
    if (phase === 'result' && !book.total()) {
      var last = S.roul.lastBets;
      if (!last) return;
      startNew();
      var err = book.load(last);
      if (err) { A.toast(err); sync(); return; }
      drawChips();
    }
    if (!book.total()) return;
    var res = BJ.rouSpin(S, book.bets);
    if (!res.ok) { A.toast(res.err === 'funds' ? 'You don’t have enough chips for that bet.' : 'Check your bets and try again.'); return; }
    res.events.forEach(function (e) { if (e.t === 'vipUp') A.noteVip(e.tier); });
    A.persist();
    A.setBal(S.profile.balance - res.ret);
    busy = true; phase = 'spin';
    highlight(null);
    clearTimeout(hideTimer);
    numEl.hidden = true;
    setBanner(null);
    wrap.classList.add('show');
    sync();
    A.sfx.flip();
    var bets = Object.assign({}, book.bets);
    animate(res.n, function () { finish(res, bets); });
  }
  function finish(res, bets) {
    var col = res.color, n = res.n;
    board.querySelectorAll('.rcell.num[data-n="' + n + '"]').forEach(function (c) { c.classList.add('hit'); });
    Object.keys(BJ.OUTSIDE).forEach(function (id) {
      if (BJ.OUTSIDE[id].indexOf(n) >= 0) { var c = board.querySelector('[data-id="' + id + '"]'); if (c) c.classList.add('hit'); }
    });
    var wins = {};
    Object.keys(bets).forEach(function (id) {
      if (res.rets[id]) wins[id] = res.rets[id];
      else A.flyAway(chipEl(id), wrap);
    });
    book.clear();
    resultBets = wins;
    drawChips(wins);
    numEl.className = 'rnum ' + col;
    numEl.textContent = n;
    numEl.hidden = false;
    var title = n + ' ' + col.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + col.slice(1);
    setBanner({ t: title, a: res.net ? A.signed(res.net) : 'Bets returned', cls: res.net > 0 ? 'win' : res.net < 0 ? 'lose' : '', pop: true });
    A.setBal(A.S.profile.balance);
    if (res.net > 0) A.sfx.win(); else if (res.net < 0) A.sfx.lose(); else A.sfx.push();
    drawRecent();
    busy = false; phase = 'result';
    sync();
    A.afterRound();
    if (overlayMq.matches) hideTimer = setTimeout(closeWheel, A.ms(2000));
  }
  function closeWheel() { wrap.classList.remove('show'); setBanner(null); }
  function setBanner(o) {
    var b = q('.banner');
    b.className = 'banner';
    if (!o) { b.innerHTML = ''; return; }
    b.innerHTML = '<div class="t">' + o.t + '</div>' + (o.a ? '<div class="a">' + o.a + '</div>' : '');
    if (o.cls) b.classList.add(o.cls);
    void b.offsetWidth;
    if (o.pop) b.classList.add('pop');
  }
  function drawRecent() {
    q('.rrecent').innerHTML = A.S.roul.recent.slice(0, 16).map(function (n, i) { return '<span class="rn ' + BJ.rouColor(n) + (i === 0 ? ' latest' : '') + '">' + n + '</span>'; }).join('') || '<span class="sub">Spin to start your history</span>';
  }
  function drawModes() {
    q('.rmodes').innerHTML = [['single', 'Single'], ['split', 'Split'], ['street', 'Street'], ['corner', 'Corner'], ['line', 'Line']].map(function (m) {
      return '<button data-m="' + m[0] + '" class="' + (mode === m[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" aria-pressed="' + (mode === m[0]) + '">' + m[1] + '</button>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function sync() {
    var S = A.S, total = book.total(), last = S.roul.lastBets, refill = BJ.canRefill(S) && !total, label = 'Spin', off = false;
    if (refill) label = 'Refill ' + A.int(BJ.tierOf(S).refill) + ' free chips';
    else if (phase === 'result' && !total && last) label = 'Spin again';
    else if (!total) { label = 'Place a bet'; off = true; }
    panel.set({
      total: total, label: busy ? 'Spinning' : label, mainOff: busy || off, locked: busy,
      off: { undo: phase === 'result' || !book.log.length, clear: !total && phase !== 'result', double: !total, rebet: !last }
    });
  }
  function restore() {
    if (busy) return;
    book.clear();
    startNew();
    var l = A.S.roul.last;
    wrap.classList.remove('show');
    if (l) {
      ballAt(l.n);
      phase = 'result';
      board.querySelectorAll('.rcell.num[data-n="' + l.n + '"]').forEach(function (c) { c.classList.add('hit'); });
      Object.keys(BJ.OUTSIDE).forEach(function (id) { if (BJ.OUTSIDE[id].indexOf(l.n) >= 0) { var c = board.querySelector('[data-id="' + id + '"]'); if (c) c.classList.add('hit'); } });
      numEl.className = 'rnum ' + l.color; numEl.textContent = l.n; numEl.hidden = false;
    } else placeWheel();
    drawRecent();
    sync();
  }
  function init() {
    root = document.querySelector('#viewRou');
    root.innerHTML =
      '<section class="table rt" aria-label="Roulette table">' +
        '<div class="rtop"><div class="rrecent"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="rwrap" id="rWrap"><div class="rstage"><canvas class="rcanvas" width="900" height="900" aria-hidden="true"></canvas><div class="rball"></div><div class="rnum" hidden></div></div></div>' +
        '<div class="rmodes" role="group" aria-label="What a tap on a number bets"></div>' +
        '<div class="rboardwrap"><div class="rboard" id="rBoard"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="banner" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>' +
      '</section><aside class="panel" aria-label="Roulette controls"></aside>';
    wheelEl = q('.rcanvas'); ballEl = q('.rball'); numEl = q('.rnum'); wrap = q('#rWrap'); board = q('#rBoard');
    drawWheel(wheelEl);
    if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(function () { drawWheel(wheelEl); });
    book = new A.Book();
    panel = A.betPanel(q('.panel'), { undo: onUndo, clear: onClear, double: onDouble, rebet: onRebet, main: onMain });
    drawModes();
    q('.rmodes').addEventListener('click', function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('[data-m]'); if (b) { mode = b.dataset.m; drawModes(); A.sfx.chip(); } });
    build();
    bindBoard();
    verticalMq.addEventListener('change', function () { build(); });
    wrap.addEventListener('click', function () { if (busy) skip = true; else if (overlayMq.matches) { clearTimeout(hideTimer); closeWheel(); } });
  }

  A.register({
    id: 'rou', init: init, restore: restore, isBusy: function () { return busy; },
    refresh: function () { panel.redraw(); if (A.S) sync(); },
    enter: function () { drawRecent(); sync(); },
    onKey: function (e) { if (e.key === ' ' && e.target.tagName !== 'BUTTON') { e.preventDefault(); onMain(); } },
    rules: function () {
      var row = function (a, b) { return '<tr><td>' + a + '</td><td>' + b + '</td></tr>'; };
      return '<div class="sec"><h3>Bets</h3><table class="pay">' + row('Single number', '35 to 1') + row('Split, two numbers', '17 to 1') + row('Street, three numbers', '11 to 1') + row('Corner, four numbers', '8 to 1') + row('Line, six numbers', '5 to 1') + row('Dozen or column', '2 to 1') + row('Red, black, even, odd, 1 to 18, 19 to 36', '1 to 1') + '</table></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>How to bet</h3><ul class="rules"><li>European wheel with a single zero. Outside bets lose on 0.</li><li>Pick a chip value, then tap the board. Chips add up on the same bet.</li><li>On a phone, choose Single, Split, Street, Corner or Line, then tap the number you want. The closest side or corner decides which numbers you get.</li><li>With a mouse, point at a line or corner between numbers. The numbers you’ll cover light up.</li><li>Tap the wheel while it spins to skip to the result.</li></ul></div>';
    }
  });
})();

</script>
<script>
(function () {
  'use strict';
