  var A = Casino, C = BJ.C;
  var root, tab = 'crypto', sel = 'aur', tf = 1, ctype = 'candle';
  var ticket = freshTicket();
  function freshTicket() { return { mode: 'lev', side: 'buy', dir: 'long', lev: 10, pct: 0, tp: 0, sl: 0, amt: '', manage: null, showAuto: false }; }
  var rows = {}, rugNoted = {}, loopOn = false;
  var TFS = [['Live', 1], ['1m', 60], ['5m', 300], ['15m', 900], ['1H', 3600]];
  function q(s) { return root.querySelector(s); }

  /* ---------- Formatting ---------- */
  function fp(p) {
    if (!isFinite(p)) return '0';
    if (p >= 1000) return p.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
    if (p >= 1) return p.toFixed(p >= 100 ? 2 : 3);
    if (p <= 0) return '0';
    if (p < 0.0001) {
      /* Show long runs of zeros the way token screens do: 0.0(5)1058 */
      var zeros = Math.ceil(-Math.log10(p)) - 1, sig = Math.round(p * Math.pow(10, zeros + 4));
      if (sig >= 10000) { sig = 1000; zeros -= 1; }
      return '0.0' + String(zeros).replace(/\d/g, function (d) { return '\u2080\u2081\u2082\u2083\u2084\u2085\u2086\u2087\u2088\u2089'[+d]; }) + sig;
    }
    return p.toFixed(Math.ceil(-Math.log10(p)) + 3);
  }
  function fq(x) {
    if (x >= 1e9) return (x / 1e9).toFixed(2) + 'B';
    if (x >= 1e6) return (x / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'M';
    if (x >= 1e4) return Math.round(x).toLocaleString('en-US');
    if (x >= 1) return x.toFixed(2);
    return x.toPrecision(3);
  }
  function fm(ch) {
    if (ch >= 1e12) return (ch / 1e12).toFixed(2) + 'T';
    if (ch >= 1e9) return (ch / 1e9).toFixed(2) + 'B';
    if (ch >= 1e6) return (ch / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'M';
    if (ch >= 1e3) return (ch / 1e3).toFixed(1) + 'K';
    return ch.toFixed(0);
  }
  function pct(x) { return (x >= 0 ? '+' : '\u2212') + Math.abs(x * 100).toFixed(2) + '%'; }
  function cls(x) { return x > 0 ? 'pos' : x < 0 ? 'neg' : ''; }
  function age(s) { var m = Math.floor(s / 60); if (m < 60) return m + 'm'; var h = Math.floor(m / 60); if (h < 48) return h + 'h'; return Math.floor(h / 24) + 'd'; }
  function ic(c) {
    return '<span class="coinic' + (c.e ? ' em' : '') + '" style="--c:' + c.c + '">' + (c.e || c.g) + '</span>';
  }
  function sparkSvg(id, up) {
    var v = MK.spark(id, 24), lo = Math.min.apply(null, v), hi = Math.max.apply(null, v), w = 54, h = 26;
    if (hi === lo) hi = lo * 1.0001;
    var pts = v.map(function (x, i) { return (i / 24 * w).toFixed(1) + ',' + (h - 2 - ((x - lo) / (hi - lo)) * (h - 4)).toFixed(1); }).join(' ');
    return '<svg class="spk" viewBox="0 0 54 26" aria-hidden="true"><polyline points="' + pts + '" fill="none" stroke="' + (up ? '#27e07a' : '#ff5a6e') + '" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>';
  }

  /* ---------- Skeleton ---------- */
  function build() {
    root = document.querySelector('#viewTrade');
    root.dataset.screen = 'list';
    root.innerHTML =
      '<section class="tl" aria-label="Markets">' +
        '<div class="tl-top"><div class="tl-title"><h2>Trade</h2><span class="simtag">Simulated market</span></div>' +
        '<button class="tpf" id="tpf"></button>' +
        '<div class="seg" id="tTabs"><button data-t="crypto">Crypto</button><button data-t="stocks">Stocks</button><button data-t="memes">Memes</button><button data-t="new">New</button><button data-t="portfolio">Portfolio</button></div></div>' +
        '<div class="tlist" id="tList"></div>' +
      '</section>' +
      '<section class="td" aria-label="Coin details">' +
        '<div class="td-head"><button class="tback" id="tBack" aria-label="Back to markets"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M10 3 5 8l5 5"/></svg></button><span id="tdIc"></span><span class="td-name"><b id="tdName"></b><small id="tdSym"></small></span><span class="live"><i></i>Live</span></div>' +
        '<div class="td-pr"><span class="big" id="tdPrice"></span><span class="chg" id="tdChg"></span></div>' +
        '<div class="rugbar" id="tdRug" hidden></div>' +
        '<div class="chartbox" id="chartBox"><canvas id="tChart" aria-label="Live price chart" role="img"></canvas></div>' +
        '<div class="tfbar"><div class="seg" id="tfSeg">' + TFS.map(function (x) { return '<button data-tf="' + x[1] + '">' + x[0] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div><button class="ctype" id="cType" aria-label="Switch between candles and line"></button></div>' +
        '<div class="tdside"><div id="tdLev"></div><div class="posc" id="tdPos" hidden></div>' +
        '<div class="stats4" id="tdStats"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="tacts"><button class="go" id="actLong">Long</button><button class="go sellb" id="actShort">Short</button><button class="act" id="actSpot">Spot</button><button class="go" id="actBuy">Buy</button><button class="go sellb" id="actSell">Sell</button></div>' +
      '</section>' +
      '<div class="tkscrim" id="tkScrim"></div>' +
      '<aside class="tk" aria-label="Order ticket">' +
        '<div class="tkhead"><b id="tkTitle"></b><button class="x" id="tkClose" aria-label="Close"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="m3 3 10 10M13 3 3 13"/></svg></button></div>' +
        '<div class="seg" id="tkMode"><button data-m="lev">Long / Short</button><button data-m="spot">Spot</button></div>' +
        '<div class="seg" id="tkSeg"><button data-s="buy">Buy</button><button data-s="sell">Sell</button><button data-s="auto">Auto-sell</button></div>' +
        '<div id="tkBody" style="display:grid;gap:12px"></div>' +
      '</aside>';
    q('#tTabs').onclick = function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('[data-t]'); if (b) setTab(b.dataset.t); };
    q('#tpf').onclick = function () { setTab('portfolio'); };
    q('#tList').onclick = function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('[data-id]'); if (b) select(b.dataset.id, true); };
    q('#tBack').onclick = function () { root.dataset.screen = 'list'; renderList(); };
    q('#tfSeg').onclick = function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('[data-tf]'); if (b) { tf = +b.dataset.tf; drawTf(); chart.reload(true); } };
    q('#cType').onclick = function () { ctype = ctype === 'candle' ? 'line' : 'candle'; drawTf(); };
    q('#actLong').onclick = function () { openLevTicket('long'); };
    q('#actShort').onclick = function () { openLevTicket('short'); };
    q('#actSpot').onclick = function () { ticket.mode = 'spot'; openTicket('buy'); };
    q('#actBuy').onclick = function () { openTicket('buy'); };
    q('#actSell').onclick = function () { if (!q('#actSell').disabled) openTicket('sell'); };
    q('#tkMode').onclick = function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('[data-m]'); if (b && !b.disabled) { ticket.mode = b.dataset.m; ticket.pct = 0; ticket.side = 'buy'; renderTicket(); } };
    q('#tdLev').addEventListener('click', onLevCard);
    q('#tkScrim').onclick = closeTicket;
    q('#tkClose').onclick = closeTicket;
    q('#tkSeg').onclick = function (e) { var b = e.target.closest('[data-s]'); if (b && !b.disabled) { ticket.side = b.dataset.s; ticket.pct = 0; renderTicket(); } };
    q('#tkBody').addEventListener('click', onTicketClick);
    q('#tkBody').addEventListener('input', function (e) { if (e.target.id === 'tkAmt') { e.target.value = e.target.value.replace(/[^\d]/g, ''); ticket.amt = e.target.value; ticket.pct = 0; highlightQuick(); updateTicket(); hideErr(); }
      if (e.target.id === 'tkLev') { ticket.lev = +e.target.value; applyLevPct(); drawLevValue(); updateTicket(); hideErr(); } });
    chart = makeChart(q('#tChart'), q('#chartBox'));
    setInterval(tick, 1000);
  }

  /* ---------- Lists ---------- */
  function setTab(t) { tab = t; if (root.dataset.screen === 'detail' && verticalLayout()) root.dataset.screen = 'list'; renderList(); }
  function verticalLayout() { return window.matchMedia('(max-width: 860px), (max-height: 520px)').matches; }
  function rowHTML(id, sub, right, pid) {
    var inf = MK.info(id), c = inf.coin;
    return '<button class="mrow' + (id === sel && !verticalLayout() ? ' on' : '') + '" data-id="' + id + '"' + (pid ? ' data-pid="' + pid + '"' : '') + '>' + ic(c) +
      '<span class="mname"><b>' + c.name + '</b><small>' + (sub || c.sym) + '</small></span>' + sparkSvg(id, inf.change >= 0) +
      '<span class="mpr">' + (right || '<b data-p></b><small data-c></small>') + '</span></button>';
  }
  function listIds() {
    var t = MK.now();
    if (tab === 'crypto') return MK.MAJORS.map(function (c) { return c.id; });
    if (tab === 'stocks') return MK.STOCKS.map(function (c) { return c.id; });
    if (tab === 'memes') return MK.MEMES.map(function (c) { return c.id; }).sort(function (a, b) { return MK.changeOver(b, 86400, t) - MK.changeOver(a, 86400, t); });
    return MK.launches(18, t).filter(function (c) { return c.launched <= t; }).map(function (c) { return c.id; });
  }
  function renderList() {
    root.querySelectorAll('#tTabs button').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.t === tab); });
    var box = q('#tList'), S = A.S, t = MK.now(), html = '';
    rows = {};
    if (tab === 'portfolio') {
      var pf = MK.portfolio(S, t);
      if (!pf.rows.length && !S.trade.lev.length) html = '<p class="tempty">You don\u2019t have any open trades yet. Open a coin and tap Long, Short or Spot to start.</p>';
      if (S.trade.lev.length) {
        html += '<div class="tsec">Open trades</div>';
        S.trade.lev.forEach(function (p) { html += rowHTML(p.id, (p.dir === 'long' ? 'Long ' : 'Short ') + p.lev + 'x, ' + A.compact(Math.round(p.margin / C)) + ' margin', '<b data-v></b><small data-pl></small>', p.pid); });
      }
      if (pf.rows.length) html += '<div class="tsec">Spot holdings</div>';
      pf.rows.forEach(function (r) {
        html += rowHTML(r.id, fq(r.qty) + ' ' + r.coin.sym, '<b data-v></b><small data-pl></small>');
      });
      var log = S.trade.log.slice(0, 16);
      if (log.length) {
        html += '<div class="tsec">Recent trades</div>' + log.map(function (l) {
          var c = MK.coin(l.id), when = new Date(l.t * 1000).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
          var lv = l.lev ? ' ' + l.side + ' ' + l.lev + 'x' : '';
          var how = l.act === 'open' ? 'Opened' + lv : l.act === 'close' ? 'Closed' + lv : l.act === 'liq' ? 'Liquidated' + lv : l.act === 'tp' || l.auto === 'tp' ? 'Take profit' + lv : l.act === 'sl' || l.auto === 'sl' ? 'Stop loss' + lv : l.side === 'buy' ? 'Bought' : 'Sold';
          return '<div class="trow"><div>' + how + ' ' + c.sym + '<small>' + when + ', ' + fq(l.qty) + ' at ' + fp(l.price) + '</small></div><div style="text-align:right"><b>' + A.fmt(l.cents) + '</b>' + (l.pnl !== undefined ? '<small class="' + cls(l.pnl) + '">' + A.signed(l.pnl) + '</small>' : '') + '</div></div>';
        }).join('');
      }
    } else {
      listIds().forEach(function (id) {
        var sub;
        if (tab === 'new') {
          var inf = MK.info(id, t);
          sub = inf.rugged ? '<span class="tagr">Rugged</span>' : age(inf.age) + ' old, cap ' + fm(inf.mcap);
        }
        html += rowHTML(id, sub);
      });
    }
    box.innerHTML = html;
    box.querySelectorAll('.mrow').forEach(function (el) { rows[el.dataset.pid ? 'P' + el.dataset.pid : el.dataset.id] = { id: el.dataset.id, pid: el.dataset.pid ? +el.dataset.pid : 0, p: el.querySelector('[data-p]'), c: el.querySelector('[data-c]'), v: el.querySelector('[data-v]'), pl: el.querySelector('[data-pl]') }; });
    updateRows(true);
    drawPortfolioCard();
  }
  var lastTxt = {};
  function setFlash(el, id, price, text) {
    if (el.textContent === text) return;
    var was = lastTxt[id];
    el.textContent = text;
    el.classList.remove('flash-up', 'flash-dn');
    if (was !== undefined && was !== price) { void el.offsetWidth; el.classList.add(price > was ? 'flash-up' : 'flash-dn'); }
    lastTxt[id] = price;
  }
  function updateRows() {
    var t = MK.now(), S = A.S;
    Object.keys(rows).forEach(function (key) {
      var r = rows[key], id = r.id, p = MK.priceAt(id, t);
      if (r.pid) {
        var lp = MK.findLev(S, r.pid);
        if (!lp) return;
        var ln = MK.pnlAt(lp, p);
        r.v.textContent = A.fmt(Math.round(Math.max(0, lp.margin + ln)));
        r.pl.textContent = pct(ln / lp.margin);
        r.pl.className = cls(ln);
        return;
      }
      if (r.p) {
        setFlash(r.p, id, p, fp(p));
        var ch = MK.changeOver(id, 86400, t);
        r.c.textContent = pct(ch);
        r.c.className = cls(ch);
      }
      if (r.v) {
        var pos = S.trade.pos[id];
        if (!pos) return;
        var val = pos.qty * p * C, pl = val - pos.cost;
        r.v.textContent = A.fmt(Math.round(val));
        r.pl.textContent = pct(pos.cost ? pl / pos.cost : 0);
        r.pl.className = cls(pl);
      }
    });
  }
  function drawPortfolioCard() {
    var pf = MK.portfolio(A.S), lv = MK.levSummary(A.S), value = pf.value + lv.equity, pnl = pf.pnl + lv.pnl, base = pf.cost + lv.margin;
    q('#tpf').innerHTML = '<span><small>Open trades value</small><b>' + A.fmt(Math.round(value)) + '</b></span><span class="pl ' + cls(pnl) + '">' + (pf.rows.length || lv.count ? A.signed(Math.round(pnl)) + ' (' + pct(base ? pnl / base : 0) + ')' : 'No open trades') + '</span>';
  }

  /* ---------- Coin page ---------- */
  function select(id, go) {
    if (id !== sel && MK.coin(id).kind === 'launch' && tf >= 300) { tf = 60; drawTf(); }
    if (id !== sel) { ticket.tp = 0; ticket.sl = 0; ticket.showAuto = false; ticket.manage = null; ticket.side = 'buy'; }
    sel = id;
    ticket.mode = MK.coin(id).maxLev ? (ticket.mode === 'spot' ? 'spot' : 'lev') : 'spot';
    ticket.pct = 0;
    if (go) root.dataset.screen = 'detail';
    drawHeader();
    drawPos();
    renderTicket();
    chart.reload(true);
    if (!verticalLayout()) root.querySelectorAll('.mrow').forEach(function (m) { m.classList.toggle('on', m.dataset.id === id); });
  }
  function drawHeader() {
    var c = MK.coin(sel);
    q('#tdIc').innerHTML = ic(c);
    q('#tdName').textContent = c.name;
    q('#tdSym').textContent = c.sym;
    updateDetail();
  }
  function updateDetail() {
    var t = MK.now(), inf = MK.info(sel, t), c = inf.coin;
    q('#tdPrice').textContent = fp(inf.price);
    var ch = c.kind === 'launch' ? MK.changeOver(sel, Math.min(86400, t - c.launched), t) : inf.change;
    var el = q('#tdChg');
    el.textContent = pct(ch) + (c.kind === 'launch' ? ' since launch' : ' 24h');
    el.className = 'chg ' + cls(ch);
    var rug = q('#tdRug');
    rug.hidden = !inf.rugged;
    if (inf.rugged) rug.textContent = 'This token was rugged. Its developer pulled the liquidity, so buying is closed. You can still sell what\u2019s left.';
    var cells;
    if (c.kind === 'launch') cells = [['Age', age(inf.age)], ['Market cap', fm(inf.mcap)], ['Developer holds', inf.dev + '%'], ['Risk', '<b class="' + inf.risk + '">' + inf.risk + '</b>']];
    else cells = [['24h high', fp(inf.high)], ['24h low', fp(inf.low)], ['Market cap', fm(inf.mcap)], ['24h volume', fm(inf.volume)]];
    var html = cells.map(function (x) { return '<div><span>' + x[0] + '</span>' + (x[1].indexOf('<b') === 0 ? x[1] : '<b>' + x[1] + '</b>') + '</div>'; }).join('');
    var st = q('#tdStats');
    if (st.innerHTML !== html) st.innerHTML = html;
    drawPos();
    var held = !!A.S.trade.pos[sel], lev = !!c.maxLev;
    q('#actSell').disabled = !held;
    q('#actBuy').disabled = inf.rugged;
    q('#actLong').hidden = q('#actShort').hidden = q('#actSpot').hidden = !lev;
    q('#actBuy').hidden = q('#actSell').hidden = lev;
    drawLevCards();
  }
  function drawPos() {
    var pos = A.S.trade.pos[sel], el = q('#tdPos');
    el.hidden = !pos;
    if (!pos) return;
    var p = MK.priceAt(sel), val = pos.qty * p * C, pl = val - pos.cost;
    var auto = (pos.tp ? 'Take profit at ' + fp(pos.tp) : '') + (pos.tp && pos.sl ? ', ' : '') + (pos.sl ? 'stop loss at ' + fp(pos.sl) : '');
    var html = '<div><span>Your position</span><b>' + A.fmt(Math.round(val)) + '</b></div><div><span>Average entry</span><b>' + fp(MK.avgEntry(pos)) + '</b></div><div><span>Profit</span><b class="' + cls(pl) + '">' + pct(pos.cost ? pl / pos.cost : 0) + '</b></div>' + (auto ? '<div class="auto">Auto-sell: ' + auto + '</div>' : '');
    el.innerHTML = html;
  }
  function drawLevCards() {
    var S = A.S, box = q('#tdLev'), list = S.trade.lev.filter(function (p) { return p.id === sel; }), t = MK.now(), price = MK.priceAt(sel, t);
    root.classList.toggle('haslev', list.length > 0);
    var sig = sel + ':' + list.map(function (p) { return [p.pid, p.margin, p.tp, p.sl].join('/'); }).join(',');
    if (box.dataset.sig !== sig) {
      box.dataset.sig = sig;
      box.innerHTML = list.map(function (p) {
        var auto = (p.tp ? 'Take profit ' + fp(p.tp) : '') + (p.tp && p.sl ? ', ' : '') + (p.sl ? 'stop loss ' + fp(p.sl) : '');
        return '<div class="lpos" data-pid="' + p.pid + '"><div class="lp-top"><span class="lp-tag ' + p.dir + '">' + (p.dir === 'long' ? 'Long' : 'Short') + ' ' + p.lev + 'x</span><b class="lp-pnl" data-f="pnl"></b><small data-f="roe"></small></div>' +
          '<div class="lp-grid"><div><span>Position</span><b>' + fm(p.notional / C) + '</b></div><div><span>Margin</span><b>' + A.plain(p.margin) + '</b></div><div><span>Entry</span><b>' + fp(p.entry) + '</b></div><div><span>Liquidation</span><b class="lq">' + fp(p.liq) + '</b></div></div>' +
          (auto ? '<div class="lp-auto">' + auto + '</div>' : '') +
          '<div class="lp-acts"><button class="act" data-a="close">Close</button><button class="act" data-a="half">Close half</button><button class="act" data-a="tpsl">TP / SL</button></div></div>';
      }).join('');
    }
    list.forEach(function (p) {
      var el = box.querySelector('[data-pid="' + p.pid + '"]'), n = MK.pnlAt(p, price);
      var a = el.querySelector('[data-f="pnl"]'), b = el.querySelector('[data-f="roe"]');
      a.textContent = A.signed(Math.round(n)); a.className = 'lp-pnl ' + cls(n);
      b.textContent = pct(n / p.margin); b.className = cls(n);
    });
  }
  function onLevCard(e) {
    var b = e.target.closest('[data-a]');
    if (!b) return;
    A.ac();
    var pid = +b.closest('[data-pid]').dataset.pid, S = A.S;
    if (b.dataset.a === 'tpsl') {
      var p = MK.findLev(S, pid);
      ticket.manage = pid; ticket.tp = p.tpRoe || 0; ticket.sl = p.slRoe || 0;
      renderTicket();
      root.dataset.ticket = 'open';
      return;
    }
    var r = MK.closeLev(S, pid, b.dataset.a === 'half' ? 0.5 : 1);
    if (!r.ok) return;
    A.toast('Closed for ' + A.fmt(r.credit) + ' (' + A.signed(r.net) + ')', r.net > 0);
    if (ticket.manage === pid) ticket.manage = null;
    afterTrade(r.events);
    renderTicket();
  }
  function drawTf() {
    root.querySelectorAll('#tfSeg button').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', +b.dataset.tf === tf); });
    q('#cType').innerHTML = ctype === 'candle'
      ? '<svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor"><rect x="3" y="6" width="4" height="9" rx="1"/><rect x="4.4" y="3" width="1.2" height="15"/><rect x="12" y="4" width="4" height="8" rx="1"/><rect x="13.4" y="1.5" width="1.2" height="14"/></svg>'
      : '<svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M2 14l5-5 4 3 7-8"/></svg>';
  }

  /* ---------- Ticket ---------- */
  function openTicket(side) { ticket.manage = null; ticket.side = side; ticket.pct = 0; if (!MK.coin(sel).maxLev) ticket.mode = 'spot'; renderTicket(); root.dataset.ticket = 'open'; }
  function openLevTicket(dir) { ticket.manage = null; ticket.mode = 'lev'; ticket.dir = dir; ticket.pct = 0; renderTicket(); root.dataset.ticket = 'open'; }
  function closeTicket() { root.dataset.ticket = ''; if (ticket.manage) { ticket.manage = null; renderTicket(); } }
  function levPresets(max) {
    var l = [2, 5, 10, 20, 40].filter(function (x) { return x < max; });
    l.push(max);
    return l;
  }
  function drawLevValue() {
    var v = q('#tkLevV'), r = q('#tkLev');
    if (v) v.textContent = ticket.lev + 'x';
    if (r) r.value = ticket.lev;
    root.querySelectorAll('[data-lv]').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', +b.dataset.lv === ticket.lev); });
  }
  function applyLevPct() {
    if (!ticket.pct) return;
    var el = q('#tkAmt'), m = Math.floor(MK.maxMargin(A.S, sel, ticket.lev) / C * ticket.pct / 100);
    ticket.amt = m > 0 ? String(m) : '';
    if (el) el.value = ticket.amt;
  }
  function autoRows(unit) {
    return '<div><h4>Take profit' + unit + '</h4><div class="presets">' + [0, 25, 50, 100, 300].map(function (x) { return '<button data-tp="' + x + '">' + (x ? '+' + x + '%' : 'Off') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>' +
      '<div><h4>Stop loss' + unit + '</h4><div class="presets">' + [0, 25, 50, 75].map(function (x) { return '<button data-sl="' + x + '">' + (x ? '\u2212' + x + '%' : 'Off') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>';
  }
  function renderLevBody() {
    var c = MK.coin(sel), max = c.maxLev, body = q('#tkBody');
    if (ticket.lev > max) ticket.lev = max;
    body.innerHTML = '<div class="seg dirseg" id="tkDir"><button data-dir="long" class="' + (ticket.dir === 'long' ? 'on' : '') + '">Long</button><button data-dir="short" class="' + (ticket.dir === 'short' ? 'on' : '') + '">Short</button></div>' +
      '<div><div class="lab"><label for="tkLev">Leverage, up to ' + max + 'x on ' + c.sym + '</label><b class="levv" id="tkLevV"></b></div><input type="range" id="tkLev" min="2" max="' + max + '" step="1" value="' + ticket.lev + '"><div class="presets lvp">' + levPresets(max).map(function (x) { return '<button data-lv="' + x + '">' + x + 'x</button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>' +
      '<div><div class="lab"><label for="tkAmt">Margin</label><span id="tkAvail"></span></div><div class="inputrow" id="tkRow"><div class="in"><span class="coinslot">' + coinSvg() + '</span><input id="tkAmt" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" placeholder="0" value="' + ticket.amt + '"></div></div></div>' +
      '<div class="quick">' + [25, 50, 75, 100].map(function (x) { return '<button data-q="' + x + '">' + (x === 100 ? 'Max' : x + '%') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="tksum" id="tkSum"></div>' +
      '<button class="sidet" id="tkAutoT" aria-expanded="' + ticket.showAuto + '"><span>Take profit and stop loss</span><span class="r"><span id="tkAutoS"></span></span></button>' +
      (ticket.showAuto ? '<div style="display:grid;gap:10px">' + autoRows(' on margin') + '</div>' : '') +
      '<p class="err" id="tkErr" hidden></p><button class="go" id="tkGo"></button>';
    drawLevValue(); drawPresets();
  }
  function renderManage() {
    var p = MK.findLev(A.S, ticket.manage);
    q('#tkBody').innerHTML = '<p class="sub">' + (p.dir === 'long' ? 'Long ' : 'Short ') + p.lev + 'x on ' + MK.coin(p.id).sym + '. Percentages are of your margin, and the trade closes automatically at the target, even while the app is closed.</p>' + autoRows('') + '<div class="tksum" id="tkSum"></div><button class="go" id="tkGo">Save</button>';
    drawPresets();
  }
  function renderTicket() {
    var c = MK.coin(sel), S = A.S, pos = S.trade.pos[sel];
    if (ticket.manage && !MK.findLev(S, ticket.manage)) ticket.manage = null;
    if (!c.maxLev) ticket.mode = 'spot';
    q('#tkTitle').textContent = c.name;
    var modeBar = q('#tkMode'), spotBar = q('#tkSeg');
    modeBar.hidden = !!ticket.manage;
    spotBar.hidden = ticket.mode !== 'spot' || !!ticket.manage;
    root.querySelectorAll('#tkMode button').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.m === ticket.mode); b.disabled = b.dataset.m === 'lev' && !c.maxLev; });
    if (ticket.manage) { renderManage(); updateTicket(); return; }
    if (ticket.mode === 'lev') { renderLevBody(); highlightQuick(); updateTicket(); return; }
    if (ticket.side !== 'buy' && !pos) ticket.side = 'buy';
    root.querySelectorAll('#tkSeg button').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', b.dataset.s === ticket.side); b.disabled = b.dataset.s !== 'buy' && !pos; });
    var body = q('#tkBody');
    if (ticket.side === 'buy') {
      body.innerHTML = '<div><div class="lab"><label for="tkAmt">Amount to spend</label><span id="tkAvail"></span></div><div class="inputrow" id="tkRow"><div class="in"><span class="coinslot">' + coinSvg() + '</span><input id="tkAmt" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" placeholder="0"></div></div></div>' +
        '<div class="quick">' + [25, 50, 75, 100].map(function (x) { return '<button data-q="' + x + '">' + (x === 100 ? 'Max' : x + '%') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="tksum" id="tkSum"></div><p class="err" id="tkErr" hidden></p><button class="go" id="tkGo"></button>';
    } else if (ticket.side === 'sell') {
      if (!ticket.pct) ticket.pct = 100;
      body.innerHTML = '<div><div class="lab"><span>Amount to sell</span><span>' + fq(pos.qty) + ' ' + c.sym + '</span></div><div class="quick">' + [25, 50, 75, 100].map(function (x) { return '<button data-q="' + x + '">' + (x === 100 ? 'All' : x + '%') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="tksum" id="tkSum"></div><p class="err" id="tkErr" hidden></p><button class="go sellb" id="tkGo"></button>';
    } else {
      ticket.tp = pos.tpPct || 0; ticket.sl = pos.slPct || 0;
      body.innerHTML = '<p class="sub">Sells your whole position automatically when the price reaches your target, even while the app is closed. Measured from your average entry.</p>' +
        '<div><h4>Take profit</h4><div class="presets" id="tkTP">' + [0, 25, 50, 100, 300].map(function (x) { return '<button data-tp="' + x + '">' + (x ? '+' + x + '%' : 'Off') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>' +
        '<div><h4>Stop loss</h4><div class="presets" id="tkSL">' + [0, 10, 25, 50].map(function (x) { return '<button data-sl="' + x + '">' + (x ? '\u2212' + x + '%' : 'Off') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="tksum" id="tkSum"></div><button class="go" id="tkGo">Save auto-sell</button>';
      drawPresets();
    }
    highlightQuick();
    updateTicket();
  }
  function coinSvg() { return '<svg class="coin" viewBox="0 0 16 16" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="8" cy="8" r="8" fill="#f6c453"/><circle cx="8" cy="8" r="5.6" fill="none" stroke="#b9851f" stroke-width="1.2"/></svg>'; }
  function drawPresets() {
    root.querySelectorAll('[data-tp]').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', +b.dataset.tp === ticket.tp); });
    root.querySelectorAll('[data-sl]').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', +b.dataset.sl === ticket.sl); });
  }
  function highlightQuick() { root.querySelectorAll('.tk .quick button').forEach(function (b) { b.classList.toggle('on', +b.dataset.q === ticket.pct); }); }
  function amtCents() { var el = q('#tkAmt'); return el ? (parseInt(el.value, 10) || 0) * C : 0; }
  function hideErr() { var e = q('#tkErr'); if (e) e.hidden = true; var r = q('#tkRow'); if (r) r.classList.remove('bad'); }
  function showErr(m) { var e = q('#tkErr'); if (e) { e.textContent = m; e.hidden = false; } var r = q('#tkRow'); if (r) r.classList.add('bad'); }
  function updateTicket() {
    var S = A.S, c = MK.coin(sel), sum = q('#tkSum'), go = q('#tkGo');
    if (!sum) return;
    var rows2 = [], pos = S.trade.pos[sel], label;
    if (ticket.manage) {
      var mp = MK.findLev(S, ticket.manage);
      if (!mp) return;
      var d = mp.dir === 'long' ? 1 : -1;
      rows2.push(['Entry', fp(mp.entry)], ['Liquidation', fp(mp.liq), true]);
      if (ticket.tp) rows2.push(['Closes in profit at', fp(mp.entry * (1 + d * ticket.tp / 100 / mp.lev))]);
      if (ticket.sl) rows2.push(['Closes at a loss at', fp(mp.entry * (1 - d * ticket.sl / 100 / mp.lev))]);
    } else if (ticket.mode === 'lev') {
      var mg = amtCents(), sim = MK.quoteLev(sel, ticket.dir, mg || C, ticket.lev), maxM = MK.maxMargin(S, sel, ticket.lev);
      q('#tkAvail').textContent = 'Available ' + A.fmt(S.profile.balance);
      rows2.push(['Entry price', fp(sim.entry)]);
      if (mg) rows2.push(['Position size', fm(sim.notional / C)]);
      rows2.push(['Liquidation price', fp(sim.liq), true], ['Liquidates on a move of', (sim.move * 100).toFixed(2) + '%', sim.move < 0.05]);
      rows2.push(mg ? ['Fee to open', A.fmt(sim.fee)] : ['Fee to open and to close', (sim.rate * 100).toFixed(2) + '% of position size']);
      var st = q('#tkAutoS');
      if (st) st.textContent = ticket.tp || ticket.sl ? (ticket.tp ? '+' + ticket.tp + '%' : '') + (ticket.tp && ticket.sl ? ' / ' : '') + (ticket.sl ? '\u2212' + ticket.sl + '%' : '') : 'Off';
      go.textContent = 'Open ' + ticket.dir + ' ' + ticket.lev + 'x';
      go.className = 'go' + (ticket.dir === 'short' ? ' sellb' : '');
      go.disabled = !mg || maxM < C;
    } else if (ticket.side === 'buy') {
      var cents = amtCents(), rug = MK.isRugged(c, MK.now());
      q('#tkAvail').textContent = 'Available ' + A.fmt(S.profile.balance);
      if (cents) {
        var b = MK.quoteBuy(sel, cents);
        rows2.push(['You get', fq(b.qty) + ' ' + c.sym], ['Price', fp(b.fill)], ['Fee (' + (c.fee * 100) + '%)', A.fmt(b.fee)]);
        if (b.impact >= 0.002) rows2.push(['Price impact', (b.impact * 100).toFixed(2) + '%', b.impact >= 0.03]);
      } else rows2.push(['Price', fp(MK.priceAt(sel))], ['Fee', (c.fee * 100) + '%']);
      label = rug ? 'Buying is closed' : 'Buy ' + c.sym;
      go.textContent = label;
      go.disabled = rug || !cents;
    } else if (ticket.side === 'sell' && pos) {
      var f = (ticket.pct || 100) / 100, qty = pos.qty * f, s = MK.quoteSell(sel, qty), cost = pos.cost * f, pnl = s.net - cost;
      rows2.push(['Selling', fq(qty) + ' ' + c.sym], ['Price', fp(s.fill)], ['Fee (' + (c.fee * 100) + '%)', A.fmt(s.fee)]);
      if (s.impact >= 0.002) rows2.push(['Price impact', (s.impact * 100).toFixed(2) + '%', s.impact >= 0.03]);
      rows2.push(['You receive', A.fmt(s.net)], ['Profit on this sale', A.signed(Math.round(pnl))]);
      go.textContent = 'Sell ' + (ticket.pct === 100 || !ticket.pct ? 'all ' : ticket.pct + '% of ') + c.sym;
      go.disabled = false;
    } else if (ticket.side === 'auto' && pos) {
      var a = MK.avgEntry(pos);
      rows2.push(['Average entry', fp(a)]);
      if (ticket.tp) rows2.push(['Sells at or above', fp(a * (1 + ticket.tp / 100))]);
      if (ticket.sl) rows2.push(['Sells at or below', fp(a * (1 - ticket.sl / 100))]);
    }
    var html = rows2.map(function (x) { return '<div' + (x[2] ? ' class="warn"' : '') + '><span>' + x[0] + '</span><b>' + x[1] + '</b></div>'; }).join('');
    if (sum.innerHTML !== html) sum.innerHTML = html;
  }
  function afterTrade(evs) {
    (evs || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e.t === 'vipUp') A.noteVip(e.tier); });
    A.persist();
    A.setBal(A.S.profile.balance);
    A.sfx.claim();
    updateDetail();
    renderList();
    drawPortfolioCard();
    chart.markers();
    A.afterRound();
  }
  function onTicketClick(e) {
    var b = e.target.closest('button');
    if (!b) return;
    A.ac();
    var S = A.S, c = MK.coin(sel);
    if (b.dataset.dir) { ticket.dir = b.dataset.dir; renderLevBody(); highlightQuick(); updateTicket(); return; }
    if (b.dataset.lv) { ticket.lev = +b.dataset.lv; applyLevPct(); drawLevValue(); updateTicket(); hideErr(); return; }
    if (b.id === 'tkAutoT') { ticket.showAuto = !ticket.showAuto; renderLevBody(); highlightQuick(); updateTicket(); return; }
    if (b.dataset.q) {
      ticket.pct = +b.dataset.q;
      if (ticket.mode === 'lev' && !ticket.manage) { applyLevPct(); highlightQuick(); updateTicket(); hideErr(); return; }
      if (ticket.side === 'buy') q('#tkAmt').value = String(Math.floor(S.profile.balance / C * ticket.pct / 100)) || '';
      highlightQuick(); updateTicket(); hideErr();
      return;
    }
    if (b.dataset.tp !== undefined) { ticket.tp = +b.dataset.tp; drawPresets(); updateTicket(); return; }
    if (b.dataset.sl !== undefined) { ticket.sl = +b.dataset.sl; drawPresets(); updateTicket(); return; }
    if (b.id !== 'tkGo' || b.disabled) return;
    if (ticket.manage) {
      MK.setLevAuto(S, ticket.manage, ticket.tp, ticket.sl);
      A.persist();
      A.toast('Take profit and stop loss saved', true);
      ticket.manage = null; ticket.tp = 0; ticket.sl = 0;
      updateDetail(); chart.markers(); root.dataset.ticket = ''; renderTicket();
      return;
    }
    if (ticket.mode === 'lev') {
      var opened = MK.openLev(S, sel, ticket.dir, amtCents(), ticket.lev, undefined, { tp: ticket.tp, sl: ticket.sl });
      if (!opened.ok) { showErr(opened.err === 'funds' ? 'You don\u2019t have enough chips for this margin and fee. Lower it or open Rewards.' : opened.err === 'lev' ? 'Choose leverage from 2x to ' + c.maxLev + 'x.' : 'Enter a margin of at least 1 chip.'); return; }
      A.toast((ticket.dir === 'long' ? 'Long ' : 'Short ') + ticket.lev + 'x opened on ' + c.sym, true);
      ticket.amt = ''; ticket.pct = 0; ticket.tp = 0; ticket.sl = 0; ticket.showAuto = false;
      afterTrade(opened.events);
      closeTicket();
      renderTicket();
      return;
    }
    if (ticket.side === 'buy') {
      var cents = amtCents(), r = MK.buy(S, sel, cents);
      if (!r.ok) {
        showErr(r.err === 'funds' ? 'You don\u2019t have enough chips. Lower the amount or open Rewards.' : r.err === 'rugged' ? 'This token was rugged, so buying is closed.' : 'Enter at least 1 chip.');
        return;
      }
      A.toast('Bought ' + fq(r.quote.qty) + ' ' + c.sym, true);
      q('#tkAmt').value = '';
      ticket.pct = 0;
      afterTrade(r.events);
      closeTicket();
      renderTicket();
    } else if (ticket.side === 'sell') {
      var s = MK.sell(S, sel, (ticket.pct || 100) / 100);
      if (!s.ok) return;
      A.toast('Sold for ' + A.fmt(s.quote.net) + ' (' + A.signed(Math.round(s.pnl)) + ')', s.pnl > 0);
      ticket.pct = 0;
      afterTrade(s.events);
      closeTicket();
      ticket.side = 'buy';
      renderTicket();
    } else {
      MK.setAuto(S, sel, ticket.tp, ticket.sl);
      A.persist();
      A.toast(ticket.tp || ticket.sl ? 'Auto-sell saved' : 'Auto-sell turned off', true);
      updateDetail();
      chart.markers();
      closeTicket();
    }
  }

  /* ---------- Chart ---------- */
  var chart;
  function makeChart(cv, box) {
    var ctx = cv.getContext('2d'), W = 0, H = 0, dpr = 1, series = [], key = '', disp = 0, target = 0, yLo = 0, yHi = 0, seeded = false, hover = null, marks = [], bucketT = 0;
    var ro = new ResizeObserver(size);
    ro.observe(box);
    function size() {
      dpr = Math.min(2.5, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
      W = box.clientWidth; H = box.clientHeight;
      cv.width = Math.round(W * dpr); cv.height = Math.round(H * dpr);
      draw();
    }
    function loadMarks() {
      var pos = A.S.trade.pos[sel];
      marks = A.S.trade.log.filter(function (l) { return l.id === sel; }).slice(0, 30);
      void pos;
    }
    function reload(hard) {
      var k = sel + '|' + tf, t = MK.now();
      if (hard || k !== key) { key = k; seeded = false; }
      series = tf === 1 ? MK.ticks(sel, 200, t) : MK.candles(sel, tf, 220, t);
      bucketT = tf === 1 ? 0 : Math.floor(t) - (Math.floor(t) % tf);
      target = MK.priceAt(sel, t);
      if (!seeded) { disp = target; }
      loadMarks();
      draw();
    }
    function pushTick() {
      var t = MK.now(), now = Math.floor(t);
      target = MK.priceAt(sel, now);
      if (tf === 1) {
        var last = series[series.length - 1];
        if (!last || last.t < now) series.push({ t: now, c: target });
        while (series.length > 240) series.shift();
      } else {
        var b = now - (now % tf);
        if (b !== bucketT) reload(false);
        else if (series.length) { var bar = series[series.length - 1]; bar.c = target; if (target > bar.h) bar.h = target; if (target < bar.l) bar.l = target; }
      }
    }
    function range(vis) {
      var lo = Infinity, hi = -Infinity, i;
      for (i = 0; i < vis.length; i++) {
        var b = vis[i], a = b.l !== undefined ? b.l : b.c, z = b.h !== undefined ? b.h : b.c;
        if (a < lo) lo = a;
        if (z > hi) hi = z;
      }
      if (!isFinite(lo)) { lo = target * 0.99; hi = target * 1.01; }
      var pad = (hi - lo) * 0.12, min = target * 0.0012;
      if (pad < min) pad = min;
      return [Math.max(lo - pad, 1e-12), hi + pad];
    }
    function fmtTime(t) {
      var d = new Date(t * 1000);
      if (tf === 1) return d.toLocaleTimeString([], { minute: '2-digit', second: '2-digit' });
      if (tf >= 3600) return (d.getMonth() + 1) + '/' + d.getDate() + ' ' + d.getHours() + 'h';
      return d.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    }
    function draw() {
      if (!W || !H) return;
      var i, t = MK.now();
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
      var R = 74, B = 22, T = 12, pw = W - R, ph = H - T - B;
      if (series.length < 1) return;
      var vis, x, slot = 0, span = 0, line = tf === 1 || ctype === 'line';
      if (tf === 1) {
        span = Math.round(pw / 3.2);
        span = Math.min(span, 200);
        vis = series.filter(function (s) { return s.t >= t - span - 2; });
        x = function (b) { return pw - 6 - (t - b.t) * ((pw - 6) / span); };
      } else {
        var n = Math.max(30, Math.floor(pw / 8));
        vis = series.slice(-n);
        slot = (pw - 8) / n;
        var shift = n - vis.length;
        x = function (b, k) { return 4 + slot * (k + shift + 0.5); };
      }
      var liveBar = { c: disp, h: Math.max(disp, vis[vis.length - 1].h || disp), l: Math.min(disp, vis[vis.length - 1].l || disp), t: vis[vis.length - 1].t, o: vis[vis.length - 1].o };
      var drawn = vis.slice(0, -1).concat([Object.assign({}, vis[vis.length - 1], liveBar)]);
      var rg = range(drawn);
      if (!seeded) { yLo = rg[0]; yHi = rg[1]; seeded = true; }
      else { yLo += (rg[0] - yLo) * 0.14; yHi += (rg[1] - yHi) * 0.14; }
      var logScale = yHi / yLo > 3;
      var f = logScale ? Math.log : function (v) { return v; };
      var y = function (v) { return T + (1 - (f(v) - f(yLo)) / (f(yHi) - f(yLo))) * ph; };
      var inv = function (py) { var k = 1 - (py - T) / ph, val = f(yLo) + k * (f(yHi) - f(yLo)); return logScale ? Math.exp(val) : val; };

      ctx.font = '600 11px Figtree, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.textAlign = 'left';
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(148,170,186,.13)'; ctx.fillStyle = '#94aaba'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      for (i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
        var gy = Math.round(T + (ph * i) / 4) + 0.5;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, gy); ctx.lineTo(pw, gy); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillText(fp(inv(gy)), pw + 8, gy);
      }
      ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic'; ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      if (tf === 1) {
        for (i = 1; i <= 3; i++) { var tt = t - (span * i) / 4; ctx.fillText(fmtTime(tt), pw - 6 - (t - tt) * ((pw - 6) / span), H - 6); }
      } else {
        var stepN = Math.max(1, Math.floor(n / 4));
        for (i = vis.length - 1 - Math.floor(stepN / 2); i >= 0; i -= stepN) ctx.fillText(fmtTime(vis[i].t), x(vis[i], i), H - 6);
      }

      var last = drawn[drawn.length - 1], first = drawn[0], base = first.o !== undefined ? first.o : first.c;
      var upAll = disp >= base;
      var col = upAll ? '#27e07a' : '#ff5a6e';
      ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, pw, H - B + 2); ctx.clip();
      if (line) {
        ctx.beginPath();
        drawn.forEach(function (b, k) { var px = x(b, k), py = y(b.c); if (k) ctx.lineTo(px, py); else ctx.moveTo(px, py); });
        var ex = x(last, drawn.length - 1);
        ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.stroke();
        ctx.lineTo(ex, H - B); ctx.lineTo(x(drawn[0], 0), H - B); ctx.closePath();
        var gr = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, T, 0, H - B);
        gr.addColorStop(0, upAll ? 'rgba(39,224,122,.2)' : 'rgba(255,90,110,.2)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
        ctx.fillStyle = gr; ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex, y(last.c), 3.6, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.fill();
      } else {
        var bw = Math.max(2.4, Math.min(11, slot * 0.68));
        drawn.forEach(function (b, k) {
          var px = Math.round(x(b, k)), up = b.c >= b.o, cc = up ? '#27e07a' : '#ff5a6e', yo = y(b.o), yc = y(b.c);
          ctx.strokeStyle = cc; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px + 0.5, y(b.h)); ctx.lineTo(px + 0.5, y(b.l)); ctx.stroke();
          ctx.fillStyle = cc;
          var top = Math.min(yo, yc), h = Math.max(1.4, Math.abs(yo - yc));
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(px + 0.5 - bw / 2, top, bw, h, Math.min(1.5, bw / 3)); ctx.fill();
        });
      }
      /* Your trades on the chart */
      marks.forEach(function (m) {
        var px;
        if (tf === 1) { if (m.t < t - span) return; px = pw - 6 - (t - m.t) * ((pw - 6) / span); }
        else { var idx = -1; for (var j = 0; j < vis.length; j++) if (vis[j].t <= m.t && m.t < vis[j].t + tf) { idx = j; break; } if (idx < 0) return; px = x(vis[idx], idx); }
        var py = y(m.price), up = m.side === 'buy' || m.side === 'long', sz = 5;
        if (m.act && m.act !== 'open') {
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fillStyle = '#10222e'; ctx.fill(); ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeStyle = m.act === 'liq' ? '#ff5a6e' : '#edf3f7'; ctx.stroke();
          return;
        }
        ctx.fillStyle = up ? '#27e07a' : '#ff5a6e'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#10222e'; ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        if (up) { ctx.moveTo(px, py + 3); ctx.lineTo(px - sz, py + 3 + sz * 1.5); ctx.lineTo(px + sz, py + 3 + sz * 1.5); }
        else { ctx.moveTo(px, py - 3); ctx.lineTo(px - sz, py - 3 - sz * 1.5); ctx.lineTo(px + sz, py - 3 - sz * 1.5); }
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
      });
      ctx.restore();

      /* Your entry and auto-sell levels */
      var pos = A.S.trade.pos[sel];
      if (pos) {
        drawLevel(MK.avgEntry(pos), 'Entry', '#c7d5df');
        if (pos.tp) drawLevel(pos.tp, 'TP', '#27e07a');
        if (pos.sl) drawLevel(pos.sl, 'SL', '#ff5a6e');
      }
      A.S.trade.lev.forEach(function (p) {
        if (p.id !== sel) return;
        drawLevel(p.entry, (p.dir === 'long' ? 'Long ' : 'Short ') + p.lev + 'x', p.dir === 'long' ? '#7fe7ad' : '#ff9aa6');
        drawLevel(p.liq, 'Liquidation', '#ff5a6e');
        if (p.tp) drawLevel(p.tp, 'TP', '#27e07a');
        if (p.sl) drawLevel(p.sl, 'SL', '#f6c453');
      });
      function drawLevel(v, name, c) {
        if (v < yLo || v > yHi) return;
        var py = Math.round(y(v)) + 0.5;
        ctx.setLineDash([3, 4]); ctx.strokeStyle = c; ctx.globalAlpha = 0.7; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, py); ctx.lineTo(pw, py); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.setLineDash([]); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
        ctx.fillStyle = c; ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom'; ctx.font = '700 10.5px Figtree, sans-serif';
        ctx.fillText(name, 6, py - 2);
      }

      /* Last price */
      var lc = (last.o !== undefined ? disp >= last.o : upAll) ? '#27e07a' : '#ff5a6e', ly = Math.max(T + 6, Math.min(H - B - 6, y(disp)));
      ctx.setLineDash([2, 3]); ctx.strokeStyle = lc; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, Math.round(ly) + 0.5); ctx.lineTo(pw, Math.round(ly) + 0.5); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
      ctx.fillStyle = lc;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(pw + 2, ly - 10, R - 4, 20, 4); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#062014'; ctx.font = '800 11px Figtree, -apple-system, sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.fillText(fp(disp), pw + R / 2, ly + 0.5);

      /* Crosshair */
      if (hover) {
        var bi = 0, bestD = Infinity;
        drawn.forEach(function (b, k) { var d = Math.abs(x(b, k) - hover.x); if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; bi = k; } });
        var hb = drawn[bi], hx = x(hb, bi);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(237,243,247,.5)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(Math.round(hx) + 0.5, T); ctx.lineTo(Math.round(hx) + 0.5, H - B); ctx.stroke();
        var label = fmtTime(hb.t) + '   ' + (hb.o !== undefined && !line ? 'O ' + fp(hb.o) + '  H ' + fp(hb.h) + '  L ' + fp(hb.l) + '  C ' + fp(hb.c) : fp(hb.c));
        ctx.font = '700 11.5px Figtree, -apple-system, sans-serif';
        var tw = ctx.measureText(label).width + 16, tx = Math.max(4, Math.min(pw - tw - 2, hx - tw / 2));
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0c1a24'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(tx, T, tw, 22, 5); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#edf3f7'; ctx.textAlign = 'left'; ctx.fillText(label, tx + 8, T + 11);
      }
    }
    function pt(e) { var r = box.getBoundingClientRect(); return { x: e.clientX - r.left }; }
    box.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' || e.buttons) hover = pt(e); });
    box.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { hover = pt(e); });
    box.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () { hover = null; });
    box.addEventListener('pointerup', function (e) { if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse') hover = null; });
    box.addEventListener('pointercancel', function () { hover = null; });
    function frame() {
      disp += (target - disp) * (A.reduced ? 1 : 0.16);
      if (Math.abs(target - disp) < target * 1e-7) disp = target;
      draw();
    }
    return { reload: reload, tick: pushTick, frame: frame, markers: function () { loadMarks(); draw(); }, resize: size };
  }

  function startLoop() {
    if (loopOn) return;
    loopOn = true;
    var lastF = 0;
    function step(ts) {
      if (A.view() !== 'trade' || document.hidden || A.paused) { loopOn = false; return; }
      if (ts - lastF >= 40) { chart.frame(); lastF = ts; }
      requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }
  function handleFills(fills) {
    if (!fills.length) return;
    fills.forEach(function (f) {
      var c = MK.coin(f.id);
      A.toast((f.kind === 'tp' ? 'Take profit sold ' : 'Stop loss sold ') + c.sym + ' for ' + A.fmt(f.net), f.pnl > 0);
      (f.events || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e.t === 'vipUp') A.noteVip(e.tier); });
    });
    A.persist();
    A.setBal(A.S.profile.balance);
    A.afterRound();
    if (A.view() === 'trade') { updateDetail(); renderList(); renderTicket(); chart.markers(); }
  }
  function handleLev(fills) {
    if (!fills.length) return;
    fills.forEach(function (f) {
      var c = MK.coin(f.id), tag = (f.dir === 'long' ? 'long ' : 'short ') + f.lev + 'x on ' + c.sym;
      if (f.kind === 'liq') { A.toast('Liquidated: your ' + tag + ' lost ' + A.fmt(-f.net)); A.sfx.lose(); }
      else A.toast((f.kind === 'tp' ? 'Take profit closed your ' : 'Stop loss closed your ') + tag + ' (' + A.signed(f.net) + ')', f.net > 0);
      (f.events || []).forEach(function (e) { if (e.t === 'vipUp') A.noteVip(e.tier); });
    });
    A.persist();
    A.setBal(A.S.profile.balance);
    A.afterRound();
    if (A.view() === 'trade') { updateDetail(); renderList(); renderTicket(); chart.markers(); }
  }
  function tick() {
    var S = A.S;
    if (!S) return;
    var t = MK.now();
    handleFills(MK.checkAuto(S, t, true));
    handleLev(MK.checkLev(S, t));
    Object.keys(S.trade.pos).forEach(function (id) {
      var c = MK.coin(id);
      if (MK.isRugged(c, t) && !rugNoted[id]) { rugNoted[id] = 1; A.toast(c.name + ' was rugged. Its price collapsed.'); if (A.view() === 'trade') { updateDetail(); renderList(); } }
    });
    if (A.view() !== 'trade' || document.hidden || A.paused) return;
    chart.tick();
    updateRows();
    drawPortfolioCard();
    updateDetail();
    updateTicket();
    if (tab === 'new' && Math.floor(t) % 30 === 0) renderList();
    startLoop();
  }
  function restore() {
    var S = A.S, t = MK.now();
    Object.keys(S.trade.pos).forEach(function (id) { if (MK.isRugged(MK.coin(id), t)) rugNoted[id] = 1; });
    handleFills(MK.checkAuto(S, t, false));
    handleLev(MK.checkLev(S, t));
    ticket = freshTicket();
    root.dataset.ticket = '';
    drawTf();
    renderList();
    select(sel, false);
  }

  A.drawTradeArt = function (el) {
    if (!el) return;
    var bars = MK.candles('aur', 3600, 34), lo = Infinity, hi = -Infinity;
    bars.forEach(function (b) { if (b.l < lo) lo = b.l; if (b.h > hi) hi = b.h; });
    var y = function (v) { return 14 + (1 - (v - lo) / (hi - lo)) * 92; }, html = '';
    bars.forEach(function (b, i) {
      var x = 8 + i * 9.2, up = b.c >= b.o, c = up ? '#27e07a' : '#ff5a6e', a = y(Math.max(b.o, b.c)), h = Math.max(1.5, Math.abs(y(b.o) - y(b.c)));
      html += '<rect x="' + (x + 2.4).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + y(b.h).toFixed(1) + '" width="1" height="' + (y(b.l) - y(b.h)).toFixed(1) + '" fill="' + c + '"/><rect x="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" y="' + a.toFixed(1) + '" width="5.8" height="' + h.toFixed(1) + '" rx="1" fill="' + c + '"/>';
    });
    el.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 330 120" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" aria-hidden="true">' + html + '</svg>';
  };

  A.register({
    id: 'trade', init: build, restore: restore,
    refresh: function () { if (A.S && A.view() === 'trade') updateTicket(); },
    enter: function () { renderList(); select(sel, false); chart.resize(); startLoop(); },
    rules: function () {
      var row = function (a, b) { return '<tr><td>' + a + '</td><td>' + b + '</td></tr>'; };
      return '<div class="sec"><h3>What this is</h3><ul class="rules"><li>A simulated market. Coins, stocks and prices are made up and don\u2019t track any real asset.</li><li>Prices run around the clock, including stocks and while the app is closed.</li><li>You trade with your casino chips, so profits and losses go straight to your balance.</li></ul></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>Long and short</h3><ul class="rules"><li>Long profits when the price goes up. Short profits when it goes down.</li><li>Margin is what you put in. Leverage multiplies your position, so a 1% move at 25x changes your margin by 25%.</li><li>Each trade is separate, and you can only lose its margin. If the price reaches the liquidation price, the trade closes and the margin is gone.</li><li>Take profit and stop loss are set as a percentage of margin, and they work while the app is closed.</li></ul></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>Maximum leverage</h3><table class="pay">' + MK.MAJORS.concat(MK.STOCKS, MK.MEMES).map(function (c) { return row(c.name, c.maxLev + 'x'); }).join('') + '</table><p class="sub">New launches are spot only.</p></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>Fees</h3><table class="pay wrap">' + row('Spot: crypto', '0.1% per trade') + row('Spot: stocks', '0.05% per trade') + row('Spot: memes and launches', '1% per trade') + row('Leverage: crypto', '0.05% of position, open and close') + row('Leverage: stocks', '0.02% of position, open and close') + row('Leverage: memes', '0.2% of position, open and close') + '</table><p class="sub">Fees earn VIP progress. Trading doesn\u2019t earn rakeback.</p></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>Meme coins</h3><ul class="rules"><li>Memes swing much harder than crypto, with sudden pumps and dumps.</li><li>A new token launches every 15 minutes. Most of them lose nearly all their value, and a few run huge.</li><li>A rug pull sends the price close to zero within minutes. Buying closes, but you can still sell what\u2019s left.</li><li>Tokens where the developer holds a large share are more likely to be rugged.</li><li>Big orders in thin markets move the price against you. The ticket shows this as price impact.</li></ul></div>' +
        '<div class="sec"><h3>Auto-sell</h3><ul class="rules"><li>Take profit and stop loss sell your whole position at your target.</li><li>They keep working while the app is closed. In a fast crash you may get a worse price than your stop.</li></ul></div>';
    }
  });
})();

</script>
<script>
/* Runs when Empire Mobile Casino is the phone app inside Empire City. City coins are the balance. */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  var P = null, A = Casino, lastBal = 0, lastW = 0, ready = false;
  try { if (window.parent !== window) P = window.parent.ECBridge || null; } catch (e) {}
  if (!P) return;
  A.embed = true;
  document.documentElement.dataset.embed = '1';
  function savedNumber(json, key) { try { return JSON.parse(json).profile[key] || 0; } catch (e) { return 0; } }
  function fromCity(d, first) {
    var S = A.S, cents = Math.round(d.coins * 100), wanted = Math.round((d.xp || 0) * 100);
    S.profile.balance = cents;
    if (wanted > S.profile.wagered) {
      S.profile.wagered = wanted;
      if (first) { var i = BJ.tierIndex(wanted); S.profile.tierSeen = i; S.profile.tierClaimed = i; }
    }
    lastBal = cents; lastW = S.profile.wagered;
    A.setBal(cents);
    A.refreshAll();
  }
  A.onPersist = function (S) {
    var dB = S.profile.balance - lastBal, dW = S.profile.wagered - lastW;
    lastBal = S.profile.balance; lastW = S.profile.wagered;
    P.push({ coins: dB / 100, wagered: dW / 100, json: JSON.stringify(S) });
  };
  A.accountSettings = function () {
    return '<div class="set"><div><b>Signed in as ' + P.name() + '</b><span class="sub">Your city coins are your balance here, and everything saves with your Empire City account</span></div></div>';
  };
  A.accountBoot = function (start) {
    var d = P.state();
    A.setAccount(P.name(), null, null);
    lastBal = d.json ? savedNumber(d.json, 'balance') : BJ.START_BALANCE;
    lastW = d.json ? savedNumber(d.json, 'wagered') : 0;
    start(d.json ? { json: d.json } : {});
    fromCity(d, !d.json);
    var b = document.querySelector('#brandBtn');
    b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Back to the city');
    b.querySelector('.mark').innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M10 3 5 8l5 5"/></svg>';
    b.querySelector('.word').innerHTML = '<b>City</b><small>Back to the streets</small>';
    b.onclick = function () { P.close(); };
    ready = true;
    P.ready(A);
  };
  A.cityOpen = function (view) {
    A.paused = false;
    if (!ready) return;
    fromCity(P.state(), false);
    A.show(view || 'lobby');
  };
  A.cityClose = function () { A.paused = true; };
  A.cityReload = async function () {
    if (!ready) return;
    var d = P.state();
    await A.adoptSave(d.json || null);
    fromCity(d, !d.json);
  };
})();

</script>
<script>Casino.boot();</script>
